Extensive experiments on the COCO dataset demonstrate that the proposed method consistently outperforms previous state-of-the-art approaches under this protocol, achieving substantial improvements on both OVIS and OSPS benchmarks.

</details>


### [34] [EGM-Det: Entropy-Guided Multimodal Adaptive Fusion for UAV RGB-IR Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11685)
*Cunzheng Fan,Dawei Yan,Guanlin Wang,Xingshuo Yang,Yupeng Jia,Jing Yang,Haokui Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EGM-Det，一个基于熵引导的多模态自适应融合框架用于RGB-红外无人机视角目标检测。通过熵先验引导偏移对齐与空间-通道门控融合，并结合跨模态蒸馏，在三个基准上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RGB-红外多模态检测方法大多使用静态或固定权重融合多模态特征，忽略了不同空间位置上模态可靠性的差异，导致异构特征被均匀融合，性能受限。

Method: EGM-Det采用双流架构保留模态专属表示，核心是Entropy Offset Gate Fusion模块：(1)从输入强度、局部熵和跨模态差异中提取浅层熵先验；(2)利用熵先验引导局部偏移对齐和空间-通道门控融合，选择性聚合可靠模态线索；(3)引入跨模态蒸馏正则化融合门，通过熵自适应监督突出不确定模态决策。

Result: 在DroneVehicle、LLVIP和VEDAI三个基准上均达到SOTA性能，其中在VEDAI上较先前方法提升超过10个百分点。

Conclusion: 基于熵引导的自适应门控融合能够有效解决多模态特征中空间变化可靠性问题，显著提升RGB-红外无人机目标检测性能。

Abstract: Joint use of RGB and infrared (IR) imagery can improve UAV-view object detection, but most existing methods fuse multimodal features with static or fixed weights and therefore overlook spatially varying modality reliability. We propose EGM-Det, an entropy-guided multimodal adaptive fusion framework for RGB-IR object detection. EGM-Det employs a dual-stream architecture to preserve modality-specific representations and introduces an Entropy Offset Gate Fusion module for adaptive multi-scale fusion. The module derives shallow entropy priors from input intensity, local entropy, and cross-modal discrepancy, and uses them to guide local offset alignment and spatial-channel gated fusion. It therefore selectively aggregates reliable RGB and infrared cues instead of uniformly combining heterogeneous features. We further introduce cross-modal distillation to regularize the learned fusion gates and reduce fusion degradation. Each student branch extracts complementary knowledge from the cross-modality teacher branch matched to the main branch, while entropy-adaptive supervision emphasizes uncertain modality decisions. Experiments on DroneVehicle, LLVIP, and VEDAI demonstrate state-of-the-art performance across all three benchmarks; in particular, EGM-Det outperforms prior approaches by more than 10 percentage points on VEDAI.

</details>


### [35] [Boundary-Enhanced Segmentation of Pig Point Clouds in Commercial Housing Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11697)
*Zhankang Xu,Fei Shi,Xiangyu Qi,Zhaoyang Wang,Mengxin Guo,Yikai Fan,Simon X. Yang,Qifeng Li,Weihong Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于边界特征分析的猪点云分割方法，采用Octree Transformer作为骨干网络，结合软距离边界伪标签监督和双向跨边界语义模块，有效解决真实猪舍环境中点云边界粘连和背景误分割问题，在分割精度和边界描绘上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实猪舍环境中，猪点云与背景结构紧密接触，导致目标边界模糊、局部粘连和背景误分割，严重影响下游点云补全和体型测量精度。现有方法缺乏对边界特征的有效建模，需要一种能够显式处理边界信息的点云分割方法。

Method: 该方法以Octree Transformer作为骨干网络，通过八叉树卷积、自注意力编码和多尺度特征融合整合局部几何细节与全局语义上下文；生成软距离边界伪标签以提供连续边界监督，并设计双向跨边界语义模块实现边界特征与语义特征的显式交互。

Result: 在综合数据集上的实验表明，所提方法在分割准确率、平均交并比和边界描绘方面显著优于多种最先进模型，有效缓解了边界粘连问题。

Conclusion: 该方法为精准畜牧业中下游点云补全和体型测量提供了可靠的点云输入，验证了边界特征分析在复杂真实场景点云分割中的有效性。

Abstract: In real pigsty environments, pig point clouds often come into close contact with background structures, resulting in blurred target boundaries, local adhesion, and background mis-segmentation. This reduces the accuracy of subsequent point cloud completion and body size measurement. To address these challenges, this study proposes a pig point cloud segmentation method based on boundary feature analysis. The proposed method adopts Octree Transformer as the backbone network and integrates local geometric details with global semantic context through octree convolution, self-attention encoding, and multi-scale feature fusion. Furthermore, soft-distance boundary pseudo-labels are generated to provide continuous boundary supervision, and a bidirectional cross-boundary semantic module is designed to enable explicit interaction between boundary and semantic features. Experiments conducted on a comprehensive dataset demonstrate that the proposed method significantly outperforms various state-of-the-art models in terms of segmentation accuracy, mean intersection over union, and boundary delineation. The results indicate that the method effectively alleviates boundary adhesion, providing reliable point cloud inputs for downstream precision livestock farming tasks.

</details>


### [36] [STAR: A Spatial-Topology Aware Routing Framework for Generalizable 3D Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11699)
*Mingwei Xing,Xinliang Wang,Yifeng Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: STAR是一个面向统一3D场景理解的时空拓扑感知路由框架，通过多属性自监督预训练锚定跨域结构先验，并设计域感知专家分支（DSR路由和EDA动态分配），在ScanNet和S3DIS上分别达到80.1%和77.2%的mIoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一3D场景理解模型受限于不同传感器模态间的拓扑差异。虽然MoE架构对多域3D理解是灵活的，但传统仅基于特征的MoE路由器在语义监督下可能无法充分表征局部采样拓扑，导致语义一致性与几何异质性并存时专家分配困难。

Method: STAR包含两个核心分支：1）多属性自监督预训练分支，覆盖拓扑和纹理变化，用于锚定跨域结构先验；2）域感知专家分支，包含两个机制——Domain-Spatial-Guided Routing (DSR)从空间上下文中捕获局部拓扑变化，以及Entropy-controlled Dynamic Allocation (EDA)根据路由不确定性调整激活专家数量。

Result: STAR在多种室内外场景任务上验证了有效性，在ScanNet验证集上达到80.1% mIoU，在S3DIS上达到77.2% mIoU，一致优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: STAR通过将稳定的跨域表示学习与自适应的专家分配相结合，有效解决了3D多域统一理解中因传感器模态拓扑差异导致的专家路由难题，验证了跨域3D场景统一建模的可行性。

Abstract: Constructing a unified 3D scene understanding model has long been hindered by the topological discrepancies across sensor modalities. While applying the Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture is a flexible approach for multi-domain 3D understanding, we observe that conventional feature-only MoE routers may underrepresent local sampling topology under semantic supervision, making expert allocation difficult when semantic consistency coexists with geometric heterogeneity. To overcome this challenge, we propose STAR (Spatial-Topology Aware Routing Framework). Specifically, we introduce a multi-attribute self-supervised pre-training branch, covering topological and textural variations, to anchor cross-domain structural priors. Building upon this, we design a domain-aware expert branch with two mechanisms: Domain-Spatial-Guided Routing (DSR), which captures local topological variations from spatial context, and Entropy-controlled Dynamic Allocation (EDA), which adjusts the number of activated experts according to routing uncertainty. Together, these branches combine stable cross-domain representation learning with adaptive expert allocation. Extensive experiments across various tasks, encompassing both indoor and outdoor scenes, demonstrate the effectiveness of STAR. It achieves 80.1% mIoU on the ScanNet validation set and 77.2% mIoU on S3DIS, consistently improving over strong baselines. Code is available at our project page (https://xmw666.github.io/STAR/).

</details>


### [37] [Advancing MLLM-based UAV Image Understanding and Reasoning: A Benchmark and a Training-Free Multi-Agent System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11738)
*Haoyu Zhang,Shuoxun Zhang,Peng Ye,Lin Zhang,Jiakang Yuan,Shenghong Yi,Yuening Wang,Tao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了UAVQA-Bench，一个包含1,500个人工标注QA对、覆盖13个公开无人机数据集、6个能力维度和16个任务的无人机航拍图像理解与推理基准；并基于评估中发现的三类失败模式，提出了UAV-MAS，一个无需训练的多智能体系统，通过领域专用感知引擎、上下文感知迭代精炼模块和难度感知自适应搜索机制，在32B开源MLLM上达到77.0%的总体准确率，超越Gemini 3 Pro 4.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于MLLM的无人机航拍图像理解与推理面临极端尺度变化、任意相机方向和目标高密度等独特挑战，但现有评估分散于各独立数据集和狭窄任务中，缺乏对无人机理解与推理能力的统一评估框架。

Method: 首先构建UAVQA-Bench基准，包含1,500个人工标注QA对，覆盖13个公开无人机数据集、6个能力维度和16个任务（含多选题和视觉定位两种格式）；系统评估多种开源/闭源MLLM及基于智能体的系统后识别出三类关键失败模式，据此提出UAV-MAS多智能体系统，包含三个核心组件：DSPE（将查询路由至任务适配的视觉工具）、CAIR（验证中间推理以防止错误累积）、DAAS（根据问题难度调整搜索深度）。

Result: UAV-MAS配32B开源MLLM在UAVQA-Bench上达到77.0%总体准确率，超越Gemini 3 Pro 4.0%；8B版本比其基础模型提升8.7%。

Conclusion: 通过统一基准的构建与多智能体架构的设计，本文有效解决了MLLM在无人机航拍图像理解中的关键缺陷，并验证了无需训练的多智能体方法在提升推理能力方面的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM)-based UAV aerial image understanding and reasoning is essential for aerial intelligence yet poses distinct challenges arising from extreme scale variation, arbitrary camera orientations, and high object density. Despite growing interest, existing evaluations remain fragmented across individual datasets and narrow tasks, leaving a critical gap in unified assessment of UAV understanding and reasoning capabilities. To fill this gap, we construct UAVQA-Bench, a benchmark of 1,500 human-annotated QA pairs drawn from 13 public UAV datasets, covering 6 capability dimensions and 16 tasks in both multiple-choice and visual grounding formats. Systematic evaluation of a broad range of open-source and closed-source MLLMs as well as agent-based systems on UAVQA-Bench identifies three key failure modes: domain-toolset mismatch, unchecked error propagation, and static reasoning. Motivated by these findings, we propose UAV-MAS, a training-free multi-agent system for MLLM-based UAV aerial image understanding and reasoning, comprising a Domain-Specific Perception Engine (DSPE) that routes queries to task-appropriate visual tools, a Context-Aware Iterative Refinement module (CAIR) that validates intermediate reasoning to curb error accumulation, and a Difficulty-Aware Adaptive Search mechanism (DAAS) that adjusts search depth to question difficulty. UAV-MAS with a 32B open-source MLLM achieves 77.0% overall accuracy on UAVQA-Bench, surpassing Gemini 3 Pro by 4.0\%, while the 8B variant improves 8.7\% over its base model.

</details>


### [38] [JieZi: A Large-Scale Expert-Audited Dataset and Benchmark for Ancient Chinese Character Exegesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11741)
*Ran Li,Huiguo He,Jiahuan Cao,Junle Liu,Hiuyi Cheng,Lianwen Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出古文解字（ACCE）任务，一个基于视觉-语言问答（VQA）的古汉字考释基准，并构建了包含50万+QA对的大规模专家审核训练数据集JieZi-Dataset和四层级评估基准JieZi-Bench，实验表明当前多模态大模型在基础识别上表现良好但在字形分析、语义推理和历时演化理解上存在不足，微调后可显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 古汉字考释需要整合视觉观察、语言分析和历史语境，但现有计算方法仅聚焦于字符识别和检索等子任务，缺乏面向完整学术考释过程的结构化数据集和基准，难以支撑综合性的学术分析。

Method: 提出ACCE这个四层级的VQA任务框架（基础字识别、字形分析、意义考释、历时演化分析），并构建两个互补资源：JieZi-Dataset通过"专家设计模板+源文本引用约束"的生成pipeline减少事实错误并辅以人工验证，形成50万+QA的训练数据；JieZi-Bench由人类专家构建和验证，从权威辞典中获取参考答案且与训练数据隔离，作为评估基准。

Result: 实验表明当前多模态大语言模型在基础识别任务上表现良好，但在字形分析、语义推理和历时理解上表现欠佳；在JieZi-Dataset上进行微调后，模型在四个层级上的性能均有显著提升。

Conclusion: ACCE任务及配套的JieZi-Dataset和JieZi-Bench填补了古汉字考释缺乏系统性VQA基准的空白，为多模态模型在该学术领域的进步提供了数据支撑和评估依据，同时揭示了现有模型在深层文字学推理上的不足。

Abstract: The scholarly exegesis of ancient Chinese characters demands integrating visual observation, linguistic analysis, and historical context. However, existing computational approaches focus narrowly on subtasks such as character recognition and retrieval, lacking the structured datasets and benchmarks required for comprehensive scholarly analysis. To address this limitation, we introduce Ancient Chinese Character Exegesis (ACCE), a vision-language question answering (VQA) task that models the scholarly exegesis process. ACCE is organized into four progressive levels: basic character identification, glyph-form analysis, meaning exegesis, and diachronic evolution analysis. To support this task, we construct two complementary resources. JieZi-Dataset is the first large-scale, expert-audited VQA training dataset for ACCE, comprising over 500K QA pairs. It is constructed via a pipeline that reduces factual errors by constraining generation with expert-designed templates and source-text references. Human verification is further applied at each key stage to ensure scholarly accuracy. JieZi-Bench is an evaluation benchmark aligned with the exegesis process, constructed and verified by human experts to ensure evaluation reliability. It consists of four levels with reference answers curated from authoritative lexicographic works held separate from the training data. Experiments on multimodal large language models show that current models perform well on basic identification but struggle with glyph analysis, semantic reasoning, and diachronic understanding. Fine-tuning on JieZi-Dataset substantially improves performance across all four levels. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/Ran00w/JieZi.

</details>


### [39] [LiveAnimate: Stable Long-Form Streaming Human Animation in Real-Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11745)
*Yuxuan Zhang,Haozhong Xiong,Yubo Huang,Jiayi Song,Jinpeng Yu,Haofan Wang,Jiaming Liu,Ruihua Huang,Liwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LiveAnimate是首个结合实时流式推理与稳定长时生成的十亿级人类动画系统，基于14B参数的视频扩散Transformer（DiT），通过两阶段训练在两张NVIDIA H100 GPU上实现19.63 FPS的流式推理，并在三分钟基准测试中保持接近恒定的感知质量和身份一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 姿态驱动的人类动画生成（从单张参考图和姿态流合成目标人物视频）具有广泛的交互式应用需求（如直播、远程呈现、虚拟化身）。然而，现有基于扩散的生成系统需要数分钟到数小时生成单个片段，无法满足实时交互的要求。

Method: 1) 两阶段训练管线：第一阶段通过Reference-Anchored Teacher-Forcing Adaptation将预训练的双向DiT适配为块因果自回归生成器；第二阶段通过Block-wise Self-Forcing Distillation将采样步数降至三步。2) 提出Pose-Retrieval Sink Attention（PR-Sink）有界KV缓存机制，包含静态汇点（锚定首块）、动态汇点（存储姿态检索的历史块）和三槽滚动窗口，使内存和每块延迟不随流长度增长而增加。3) 结合Ulysses序列并行和算子融合优化推理效率。

Result: 在两张NVIDIA H100 GPU上实现19.63 FPS的流式推理。在三分钟基准测试中，从第30秒到最后一分钟保持近乎恒定的感知质量和身份一致性（IQA 4.047 vs 4.026），而先前系统在此过程中显著退化或需要数小时离线计算。该结果建立了交互式全身动画在质量、延迟和时长方面的新操作点。

Conclusion: LiveAnimate通过架构适配、自提升蒸馏和有界KV缓存（PR-Sink）三种核心设计，首次在十亿参数规模上实现实时流式长时人类动画生成，确立了交互式全身动画在质量、延迟和时长上的新操作基准。

Abstract: Pose-driven human animation synthesizes a video of a target person from a single reference image and a driving pose stream. Real-time generation is essential for interactive applications such as live streaming, telepresence, and virtual avatars, yet diffusion-based systems require minutes to hours per clip, precluding responsive interaction. We present LiveAnimate, to our knowledge the first animation system to combine real-time streaming with stable long-form generation at billion scale, built on a 14B-parameter video Diffusion Transformer (DiT). A two-stage training pipeline first adapts a pretrained bidirectional DiT into a block-causal autoregressive generator through Reference-Anchored Teacher-Forcing Adaptation, and then reduces the sampling budget to three steps through Block-wise Self-Forcing Distillation. To preserve appearance over extended streams, we introduce Pose-Retrieval Sink Attention (PR-Sink), a bounded KV-cache mechanism combining a Static Sink that permanently anchors the first generated block, a Dynamic Sink that holds a pose-retrieved historical block, and a three-slot Rolling Window. When a pose recurs, PR-Sink restores the relevant appearance context without retaining the entire sequence, so memory and per-block latency remain constant regardless of stream duration. Together with Ulysses sequence parallelism and operator fusion, these designs enable 19.63\,FPS streaming inference on two NVIDIA H100 GPUs. On a three-minute benchmark, LiveAnimate maintains nearly constant perceptual quality and identity from the first 30 seconds to the final minute (IQA 4.047 vs.\ 4.026), while prior systems degrade substantially or require hours of offline computation for the same rollout. These results establish a new operating point in quality, latency, and duration for interactive full-body animation.

</details>


### [40] [Making Every Step Count: Spatio-Temporal Information Allocation for Imaging Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11747)
*Yi Cao,Xiangyong Cao,Pei Liu,Yong-Jin Liu,Deyu Meng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出两种无需训练的即插即用模块——光谱自适应调度(SAS)和测量优先注意力(MPA)，分别从时间维度和空间维度改善基于流的生成模型在无训练逆问题求解中的NFE分配与信息传播，显著提升超分辨率、运动去模糊和修复等任务的恢复质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于流的逆问题求解器主要关注单个更新的设计，忽略了在固定NFE下时空信息分配的优化。时间上，早期探索不足会陷入错误的语义空间，而早期分配过多NFE又会导致后期细化预算不足；空间上，数据一致性约束仅作用于观测区域，缺失区域的恢复主要依赖生成先验，缺乏有效的信息引导。

Method: 提出两个互补的无训练组件：(1) 光谱自适应调度(SAS)，根据退化光谱和logSNR几何形状在流时间上分配可用的NFE，以平衡语义探索和细节细化；(2) 测量优先注意力(MPA)，利用数据与先验之间的冲突来引导信息流向约束较弱的区域，增强语义和结构保真度。两个组件均以即插即用方式集成到现有基于流的逆求解器中。

Result: 在超分辨率、运动去模糊和修复等标准图像逆问题上，所提出的组件可以即插即用地集成到现有基于流的逆求解器中，无需重新训练或额外的流模型评估，显著提高了现有求解器的恢复质量。

Conclusion: 通过SAS和MPA分别从时间维度(NFE调度)和空间维度(信息传播)优化基于流的无训练逆问题求解，是一种有效的通用增强方案，且具有良好的兼容性和实用性。

Abstract: Flow-based generative models have emerged as powerful image priors for training-free inverse problem solving, capturing coherent semantics and fine-grained structure. Despite these strengths, existing flow-based inverse solvers primarily focus on the design of individual updates, largely overlooking spatio-temporal information allocation under a fixed number of function evaluations (NFEs). Temporally, insufficient early exploration can trap the flow trajectory in an incorrect semantic basin, whereas excessive allocation of NFEs to early stages leaves little budget for late-stage refinement. Spatially, data consistency provides direct constraints only within observed regions, whereas the recovery of missing regions relies mainly on the generative prior. To address these two issues, we introduce two complementary and training-free components, i.e., Spectrum-Adaptive Scheduling (SAS) and Measurement-Prioritized Attention (MPA). For temporal allocation, SAS distributes the available NFEs over flow time according to the degradation spectrum and logSNR geometry, thus better balancing semantic exploration and detail refinement. For spatial propagation, MPA exploits data-prior conflicts to guide information toward weakly constrained regions, thereby enhancing semantic and structural fidelity. Extensive experiments on standard image inverse problems, e.g., super-resolution, motion deblurring, and inpainting, demonstrate that the proposed components can be integrated into existing flow-based inverse solvers in a plug-and-play manner without retraining or additional flow-model evaluations, and can also significantly improve the restoration quality of existing solvers.

</details>


### [41] [Dual Modality Prompted Diffusion Priors for Zero Shot Hyperspectral Pansharpening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11748)
*Pengwei Xie,Fei Zhu,Jiajun Li,Xiangyuan Liu,Xiangyuan Liu,Kangqing Shen,Gemine Vivone*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双模态图像提示扩散模型（DIDM），用于零样本高光谱全色锐化。将低分辨率高光谱和全色图像分别编码为光谱和空间提示token注入冻结的遥感扩散模型，并引入全色引导的加权像素感知总变分正则化，实现空间细节与光谱保真的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的方法仅通过外部重建目标施加观测约束，限制了全色和高光谱观测与扩散先验的直接交互，导致空间细节和光谱信息利用不足。

Method: 1) 将LRHS和PAN图像编码为光谱提示token和空间提示token，通过交叉注意力注入冻结遥感扩散模型的中间特征；2) 引入全色引导的加权像素感知总变分正则化，结合LRHS退化保真度与PAN响应保真度，并通过梯度自适应结构正则化保持结构不连续性、抑制均匀区域伪变化。

Result: 在Pavia、Chikusei和Houston数据集降分辨率协议下，所有评估指标均达到最优；全分辨率FR1上HQNR指标优于对比方法。

Conclusion: 内部双模态提示和全色引导结构正则化能在空间细节增强与光谱保真之间提供有效平衡，验证了DIDM在零样本高光谱全色锐化中的有效性。

Abstract: Hyperspectral pansharpening aims to reconstruct a high resolution hyperspectral (HRHS) image from a panchromatic (PAN) image and a low resolution hyperspectral (LRHS) image while preserving both spatial details and spectral fidelity. Recent diffusion based methods exploit pretrained image priors by generating a low dimensional representation and subsequently mapping it to the hyperspectral domain. However, the observed panchromatic and hyperspectral images are typically imposed only through external reconstruction objectives, limiting their direct interaction with the diffusion prior. To address this issue, we propose dual-modality image-prompted diffusion model (DIDM) for zero shot hyperspectral pansharpening. DIDM encodes the low resolution hyperspectral and panchromatic observations into spectral and spatial prompt tokens, respectively, and injects them into intermediate features of a frozen remote sensing diffusion model through cross attention, allowing complementary spectral and spatial information to directly guide diffusion feature evolution. In addition, we introduce a panchromatic guided weighted pixel aware total variation regularizer that combines low resolution hyperspectral degradation fidelity and panchromatic response fidelity with gradient adaptive structural regularization, thereby preserving structural discontinuities while suppressing spurious variations in homogeneous regions. Extensive experiments on Pavia, Chikusei, and Houston under reduced resolution protocols show that DIDM achieves the best performance across all evaluated metrics, while full resolution evaluation on FR1 yields the highest HQNR among the compared methods. These results demonstrate that internal dual modality prompting and panchromatic guided structural regularization provide an effective balance between spatial detail enhancement and spectral preservation.

</details>


### [42] [UniSwap: Streaming Audio-Visual Identity Swapping for Talking Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11752)
*Yuxuan Zhang,Haozhong Xiong,Jiayi Song,Jinpeng Yu,Yang Shi,Jiaming Liu,Ruihua Huang,Liwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniSwap是首个用于视频流式联合音视频身份替换的框架，通过单一音视频扩散Transformer同时迁移参考外观和音色，利用swap-and-reconstruct流水线解决跨身份训练数据稀缺问题，并引入多项优化技术实现高效流式生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有说话视频身份替换方法对视觉和音频两个模态使用独立优化的模型，难以实现音频-视频一致性的统一约束。需要一种能够同时迁移外观和声音、并保证音视频同步性的统一框架。

Method: 1) 采用单一音视频扩散Transformer实现联合身份替换；2) 提出swap-and-reconstruct流水线，通过移除真实视频中的视觉和语音身份并使用原始视频作为重建目标来解决对齐训练对稀缺问题；3) 从双向骨干网络出发，依次通过In-context Pretraining（联合替换）、Conditional Streaming Adaptation（块因果KV缓存生成）和Efficient Self-forcing DMD（缓解曝光偏差，采样步数从30降至3）进行渐进适配；4) 提出Efficient Multi-LoRA Switching让三种DMD角色共享单一冻结骨干；5) 提出Feature-RoPE Decomposition保持缓存位置在训练范围内，支持稳定长序列生成。

Result: 实验证明了强音视频同步性、有竞争力的身份保持能力、高效的流式生成和稳定的长序列生成。

Conclusion: UniSwap通过统一框架实现了说话视频中的联合音视频身份替换，在音视频同步、身份保持、流式效率和长序列稳定性方面均表现出色。

Abstract: Talking-video character replacement requires coordinated transfer of appearance and voice while preserving the source motion, scene, linguistic content, and audio-video timing. Existing methods use separately optimized models for the two modalities, making audio-visual consistency difficult to enforce. We present UniSwap, the first framework for streaming joint audio-visual identity replacement in talking videos. Given a source video, a reference image, and a reference voice clip, UniSwap transfers the reference appearance and vocal timbre within a single audio-visual diffusion transformer while preserving the source content and dynamics. To address the scarcity of aligned cross-identity training pairs, we introduce a swap-and-reconstruct pipeline that removes visual and vocal identity from real clips and uses the original clips as reconstruction targets. Starting from a bidirectional backbone, we progressively adapt the model through In-context Pretraining for joint replacement, Conditional Streaming Adaptation for block-causal KV-cached generation, and Efficient Self-forcing DMD for mitigating exposure bias and reducing sampling from 30 to 3 denoising steps per block. Efficient Multi-LoRA Switching enables the three DMD roles to share a single frozen backbone. Feature-RoPE Decomposition keeps cached positions within the training range, supporting stable long-form inference. Experiments demonstrate strong audio-visual synchronization, competitive identity preservation, efficient streaming, and stable long-form generation.

</details>

*Giancarlo Sportelli,Nicola Belcari,Roberta Pace,Umberto Bernardo,Sharmin Sultana,Alessandra Toncelli,Matteo Giaccone*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无损X射线成像可以揭示外部检查难以发现的榛子内部缺陷，但自动解读面临类别间放射影像差异细微、类别高度不平衡以及标注数据有限等挑战，需要一个系统的基准来评估不同方法。

Result: 最优方法为BCE训练的CNN与冻结Swin Transformer的平均概率集成，取得86.3%±1.8%的平均平衡准确率；多种集成方法性能相当；方法间存在显著的分割间变异性，表明多分割评估对模型可靠比较不可或缺；专家对模糊样本的重新标注使全部17种方法的性能提升2.8-8.1个百分点，但对跨分割方差影响有限。

Conclusion: 深度学习在基于X射线的自动化榛子质量评估中展现出潜力；在小型不平衡农业成像数据集中，严谨的评估协议（多分割）和标签质量审查（专家重新标注）至关重要。

Abstract: Non-destructive X-ray imaging can reveal internal hazelnut defects that are difficult to detect by external inspection alone; however, automated interpretation remains challenging because of subtle radiographic differences among classes, marked class imbalance, and limited annotated data. Here, we present a benchmark for binary hazelnut quality classification (healthy versus defective) based on 799 segmented single-kernel X-ray images (224 x 224 pixels, grayscale), grouped into 101 acquisition units. Seven single-model configurations and ten probability-aggregation ensembles were evaluated using a group-wise split-rotation protocol across five data splits generated using different random seeds. Decision thresholds were selected on the validation set, and performance was assessed deterministically on validation and test sets. Under the expert-reassessed annotation condition, the average-probability ensemble of the binary cross-entropy-trained convolutional neural network and frozen Swin Transformer achieved the highest mean balanced accuracy (86.3% +/- 1.8%, five seeds), with several other ensembles providing comparable performance. Across methods, substantial split-to-split variability was observed, indicating that multi-split evaluation is essential for reliable model comparison at this dataset scale. Expert reassessment of ambiguous samples improved the performance of all 17 evaluated methods by 2.8-8.1 percentage points, while having only a limited effect on cross-split variance. The results highlight both the potential of deep learning for automated X-ray-based hazelnut quality assessment and the importance of rigorous evaluation and label curation in small, imbalanced agricultural imaging datasets.

</details>


### [44] [ProBAG: Prototype-Guided Boundary-Aware Graph Diffusion for Weakly Supervised Histopathology Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11765)
*Duy-Dong Nguyen,Le-Van Thai,Hoai Nhan Pham,Ngoc Lam Quang Bui,Tam Tran,Zhi Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出ProBAG，一种基于原型和图像级标注的弱监督语义分割方法，用于组织病理组织分割。ProBAG结合数据集特定视觉原型与病理对齐的CONCH文本原型，在冻结的UNI多尺度特征上进行伪掩码生成。其核心包含两类互补机制：类级功率重校准（重塑类间竞争同时保留逐像素总前景激活质量）和单步图扩散（利用late-transformer注意力上下文差异作为软结构边界线索惩罚特征亲和度）。生成的阶段一伪掩码无需CRF或外部分割模型，若需完整两阶段比较，可进一步监督下游Phikon-FPN分割器。在BCSS-WSSS和LUAD-HistoSeg上的实验显示优于近期WSSS方法，消融实验表明病理对齐文本语义贡献最大，图细化提供较小的互补增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱监督病理组织分割仅使用图像级标注，避免专家逐像素标注的高昂成本。但基于CAM的方法往往只定位最具判别性的区域，且在组织界面附近不可靠，导致分割质量受限。作者旨在构建一个更优的阶段一伪掩码生成器，以提升下游分割性能。

Method: 提出ProBAG框架：1)在冻结的UNI多尺度特征上，结合数据集特定视觉原型与病理对齐的CONCH文本原型生成伪掩码；2)引入类级功率重校准机制，重塑类间竞争同时保持逐像素总前景激活量；3)采用单步图扩散，利用late-transformer注意力上下文差异作为软结构边界线索惩罚特征亲和度。生成的伪掩码无需CRF或外部分割模型，并可可选地监督下游Phikon-FPN分割器进行完整两阶段训练。

Result: 在BCSS-WSSS和LUAD-HistoSeg两个数据集上，ProBAG在弱监督语义分割任务上一致优于近期WSSS方法。消融实验显示：病理对齐的文本语义贡献最大（最大性能提升来源），而图细化机制提供较小但互补的性能增益。

Conclusion: ProBAG有效地通过视觉-文本原型结合、类级功率重校准和基于注意力的图扩散，改善了弱监督病理组织分割的伪掩码质量。病理对齐的文本语义是核心优势，图细化作为补充机制进一步提升边界精度。方法无需CRF或外部分割模型即可生成可靠的阶段一伪掩码，代码已开源。

Abstract: Weakly supervised semantic segmentation enables histopathology tissue segmentation from image-level annotations, avoiding costly pixel-level labeling by expert pathologists. However, CAM-based methods often localize only highly discriminative regions and remain unreliable near tissue interfaces. We propose ProBAG, a stage-1 pseudo-mask generator that combines dataset-specific visual prototypes with pathology-aligned CONCH text prototypes over multi-scale frozen UNI features. ProBAG introduces two complementary mechanisms: class-wise power recalibration that reshapes inter-class competition while preserving the total foreground activation mass at each pixel, and one-step graph diffusion in which feature affinities are penalized by a late-transformer attention-context discrepancy used as a soft structural boundary cue. The resulting stage-1 pseudo-masks require neither CRF nor an external segmentation model; for complete two-stage comparison, they additionally supervise a downstream Phikon-FPN segmenter. Experiments on BCSS-WSSS and LUAD-HistoSeg show consistent gains over recent WSSS approaches, while ablations indicate that pathology-aligned text semantics provide the largest improvement and graph refinement provides a smaller complementary gain. The code is available at: https://github.com/wterrr/WSSS

</details>


### [45] [Achieving Near-Zero-Overhead Multi-Model Hierarchical Classification in Real-Time Detection Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11770)
*Vaishnav Raju*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套五步方法论，实现了在NVIDIA Jetson DLA神经加速器上零GPU回退的INT8分类骨干网络部署，并通过GPU检测与DLA分类的并发流水线，在Jetson Orin NX上实现近乎零额外开销（12.5 vs 13.3 FPS）的双头人物属性分类推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘部署的视觉系统需要层次化推理流水线（检测模型+下游细粒度分类器），但将所有模型在GPU上串行运行会产生瓶颈，限制实时吞吐量。现代边缘SoC虽配备了可与GPU并发执行的NPU/DLA加速器，但由于严格的算子约束、量化不兼容和不完整的端到端流水线文档，在这些加速器上部署自定义模型仍不切实际。

Method: 提出五步方法论：（1）架构适配以符合DLA算子约束；（2）手动动态范围方案补救TensorRT隐式量化（将准确率从75%恢复至94%），用于快速流水线验证；（3）量化感知训练；（4）ONNX图手术以适配DLA编译；（5）构建GPU检测与DLA分类的并发推理流水线。同时记录九项工程约束及根因分析和通用解决方案。

Result: 在Jetson Orin NX上，双头人物属性分类器运行于DLA、GPU物体检测器运行于GPU时，流水线开销近乎为零（12.5 vs. 检测器单独的13.3 FPS，1080p），双DLA扩展无需额外成本。手动动态范围方案从隐式量化的75%准确率恢复到94.0%。

Conclusion: 该方法是骨干网络无关的，可推广到任何检测-分类边缘流水线场景，为边缘AI异构计算资源的高效利用提供了切实可行的部署路径。

Abstract: Edge-deployed vision systems in target recognition, surveillance, autonomous vehicles, and drone domains require hierarchical inference pipelines where a detection model identifies objects of interest and downstream classifiers provide fine-grained attribute analysis. Running all models on the GPU creates a serial bottleneck that limits real-time throughput as pipeline stages grow. Modern edge SoCs pair GPUs with dedicated neural accelerators (NPUs, DLAs) capable of concurrent execution, yet deploying custom models on these accelerators remains impractical due to strict operator constraints, quantization incompatibilities, and an undocumented end-to-end pipeline. We target NVIDIA Jetson DLA cores as the representative platform. We present a five-step methodology for zero GPU fallback DLA INT8 deployment of classification backbones, comprising architecture adaptation, manual dynamic range workaround to rescue TensorRT's implicit quantization (recovering 94.0% accuracy from implicit quantization's 75%) for rapid pipeline validation before explicit quantization, quantization-aware training, ONNX graph surgery for DLA compilation, and a concurrent GPU-detection/DLA-classification inference pipeline. We document nine engineering constraints with root-cause analysis and generalizable solutions. Validation on a dual-head person attribute classifier running on DLA alongside a GPU object detector on a Jetson Orin NX demonstrates near-zero pipeline overhead (12.5 vs. 13.3~FPS detector-only at 1080p), with dual-DLA scaling at no additional cost. The methodology is backbone-agnostic and generalizes to any detection-classification edge pipeline.

</details>


### [46] [VOLA: Improving Open-World Driving by VLM-Based Semantic Attribute Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11777)
*Yuchen Zhang,Yuan Gao,Sebastian Schmidt,Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从"类别标签"转向"稠密动作相关属性"的开放世界驾驶感知方法。利用Qwen3.5图像token的隐藏状态作为空间语义表示，通过轻量级边界感知解码器生成高分辨率的可驾驶性和脆弱性属性图，无需自回归文本生成或SAM等外部掩码模型。在CARLA训练并迁移到真实场景和未见过的障碍物上，达到69.4%的平均脆弱性等级召回率，优于视觉专用分割器(57.1%)和提示式VLM分割器(53.9%)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界驾驶是开放世界场景，车辆可能遇到训练数据之外的物体（如掉落的床垫、鹿等）。仅仅为这些物体命名是不够的，系统必须知道每个区域应如何影响运动决策：能否驶过？碰撞严重程度如何？因此需要将场景感知从类别标签转移到稠密的动作相关属性。

Method: 1) 将像素级标签定义为有序属性：7级可驾驶性和5级脆弱性。2) 直接读取Qwen3.5图像token的隐藏状态作为空间语义表示，避免自回归文本生成。3) 使用轻量级边界感知解码器将粗粒度的token网格转换为锐利的全分辨率属性图。4) 不使用SAM等外部掩码模型。5) 在CARLA中构建密集属性标签进行训练。

Result: 在真实开放世界异常场景迁移中，该方法达到69.4%的平均脆弱性等级召回率，远超最佳视觉专用分割器基线的57.1%和最佳提示式VLM基线的53.9%。在已熟悉类别上，性能与强视觉专用分割器相当。

Conclusion: VLM图像token能够为训练词汇之外的物体提供有用的语义线索，用于迁移驾驶属性，证明了大语言模型的视觉表示在开放世界驾驶感知中的价值。

Abstract: Driving in the real world is open-world: a car may encounter a fallen mattress, a deer, or other objects outside its training data. Naming them is not enough. The system must know how to treat each region: can it drive over it, and how severe would a collision be? We therefore shift scene perception from category labels to dense action-relevant attributes, where each pixel is labeled by how it should affect motion rather than by object name. We instantiate this general formulation with two ordered attributes: 7-rank drivability and 5-rank vulnerability. We read Qwen3.5 image-token hidden states directly as a spatial semantic representation. A lightweight boundary-aware decoder then turns this coarse token grid into sharp full-resolution attribute maps. The whole process requires neither autoregressive text generation nor an external mask model such as SAM. We train on dense attribute labels built in CARLA and test transfer to real scenes and to novel obstacles never seen in training. We compare with vision-only segmenters trained on the same attributes and prompted VLM segmenters. Our model matches strong vision-only segmenters on familiar categories and improves transfer to real open-world anomalies, reaching 69.4% mean vulnerability-rank recall versus 57.1% for the best vision-only baseline and 53.9% for the best prompted VLM baseline. These results show that VLM image tokens provide useful semantic cues for transferring driving attributes to objects outside the training vocabulary.

</details>


### [47] [Anti-Shortcut Distillation via Temporal Negative Knowledge Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11789)
*Syed Muhammad Raza,Omer Tariq,Jeongbae Son*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出反捷径蒸馏（ASD）框架，利用教师优化轨迹中早期检查点与收敛教师之间的特征位移来识别并抑制学生需要避免的捷径方向，通过时间对比损失和捷径抑制损失实现推拉式蒸馏，在多个基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识蒸馏方法只关注学生向收敛教师"吸引"（push），而忽略了教师自身在学习过程中抑制了哪些方向。作者观察到，早期教师强调而收敛教师衰减的特征，正是需要学生避免的捷径方向。这一信号已隐含在教师的优化轨迹中，但此前未被利用。

Method: ASD框架包含两个互补损失：(1) 时间对比损失（Ltc）— 将早期教师特征作为同样本负样本，与批次内及记忆库中的收敛教师特征构成InfoNCE目标；(2) 捷径抑制损失（Lss）— 惩罚学生投影到早晚期教师特征位移的未中心二阶矩矩阵E[ΔΔ^T]的顶部特征向量上。整体构成push-pull机制：向收敛教师（正向语义锚点）拉近，同时远离早期教师（时间负参考）。

Result: 在CIFAR-100、ImageNet-100和TinyImageNet上的13个教师-学生配对中，ASD在超过10个配对上取得最高干净top-1精度，并在12个配对上优于标准KD。在CIFAR-100-C对抗鲁棒性测试中，ASD在最难的跨架构配对（WRN-40-2→ShuffleNet-V2）上获得最低平均损坏误差（86.1 mCE）。机制诊断证实ASD学生与捷径方向系统性地反对齐，且在鲁棒子空间上的投影显著更大（0.45 vs 0.12）。

Conclusion: ASD验证了利用教师优化轨迹作为蒸馏信号的可行性：通过识别教师自身衰减的方向（捷径），可以更精准地引导学生远离脆弱特征，同时提升干净准确率和鲁棒性。这一时间维度的负样本思路为知识蒸馏提供了新的设计空间。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation (KD) trains a compact student by attracting it towards a converged teacher. It is silent about which directions the teacher itself learned to suppress: repulsive and bias-aware objectives exist, but none exploits the teacher's own trajectory to identify what the student should avoid. We observe that the missing signal is already encoded in the teacher's optimization trajectory: features that an early-stage teacher emphasizes but that a converged teacher attenuates are precisely the shortcut directions worth pushing the student away from. We instantiate this observation as \textbf{A}nti-\textbf{S}hortcut \textbf{D}istillation (ASD), a push--pull KD framework that treats the converged teacher $\Tfinal$ as a positive semantic anchor and an early-checkpoint teacher $\Tearly$ as a temporal negative reference. ASD couples two losses: a temporal contrastive loss ($\Ltc$) that places the early-teacher feature as a same-sample negative against in-batch and memory-bank final-teacher features in an InfoNCE objective; and a shortcut suppression loss ($\Lss$) that penalizes student projection onto the top eigenvectors of $\E[\Dh\Dh^{\top}]$, the uncentered second-moment matrix of early-to-final feature displacements. Across 13 teacher--student pairs on CIFAR-100, ImageNet-100, and TinyImageNet, ASD attains the highest clean top-1 accuracy on more than 10 pairs and outperforms standard KD on 12. On CIFAR-100-C corruption robustness, ASD obtains the lowest mean Corruption Error ($86.1$\,mCE) on the most challenging cross-architecture pair (WRN-40-2$\to$ShuffleNet-V2). Mechanistic diagnostics confirm the intended geometry: the ASD student is systematically anti-aligned with the shortcut direction, while its projection onto the robust subspace is substantially larger ($0.45$ vs.\ $0.12$).

</details>


### [48] [PolarSym: Polar Geometry-aware Attention for CAD Floorplan Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11793)
*Kerui Chen,Yiqing Wang,Kangzhou Xin,Qinghan Zhang,Songyang Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PolarSym，一种基于极坐标的几何感知注意力框架用于CAD图纸解析。该框架将建筑布局的几何关系解耦为方向和距离两个互补分量独立建模，通过动态门控机制融合，在保持原始Transformer架构的同时增强几何建模能力，在公开数据集上以极低额外计算开销超越SymPoint V2基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的CAD平面图解析方法通过自注意力捕获全局语义依赖，但仅从语义特征推断空间关系，未显式建模建筑布局固有的几何对称性，导致在长距离匹配和复杂对称空间布局中产生不匹配的对应关系。

Method: 提出极坐标几何感知注意力框架PolarSym，将建筑几何关系解耦为方向与距离两个独立建模的互补分量。方向约束强化结构一致性，距离约束建立长距离对称对应关系；采用动态门控机制协同融合两个几何信息分支，同时保持原始Transformer架构不变，从而实现以极小额外计算开销增强几何建模能力。

Result: 在公开CAD平面图解析数据集上，PolarSym在相同训练设置下较复现的SymPoint V2基线在PQ上提升1.73%，RQ提升1.54%，mIoU提升4.31%；同时收敛更快、优化更稳定。消融实验验证了方向与距离建模的互补作用。

Conclusion: PolarSym以低计算成本显著提升Transformer的几何感知能力，为CAD平面图解析提供了一种有效的几何建模范式。

Abstract: CAD plan parsing is a fundamental task in Building Information Modeling (BIM), aiming to automatically extract architectural elements including walls, doors, windows, and furniture from 2D engineering drawings. Existing Transformer-based methods capture global semantic dependencies via self-attention, yet they infer spatial relationships merely from semantic features without explicitly characterizing the intrinsic geometric symmetry of building layouts. Such methods tend to produce mismatched correspondences in long-range matching and complex symmetric spatial layouts. To tackle this limitation, we propose PolarSym, a polar-coordinate geometry-aware attention framework for CAD plan parsing. The framework decouples geometric relationships of buildings into two complementary components, direction and distance, which are modeled independently. Structural consistency is strengthened by directional constraints, while long-range symmetric correspondences are built with distance constraints. A dynamic gating mechanism is adopted to synergistically fuse the two geometric information branches while maintaining the vanilla Transformer architecture. This design boosts geometric modeling capacity with negligible extra computation. Experiments on a public CAD plan parsing dataset show that PolarSym surpasses the reproduced SymPoint V2 baseline by 1.73% PQ, 1.54% RQ and 4.31% mIoU under identical training settings. PolarSym also converges faster and yields more stable optimization. Ablation experiments verify the complementary effects of direction and distance modeling. Our results reveal that PolarSym improves the geometric awareness of Transformers at low computational cost, offering an effective geometric modeling paradigm for CAD plan parsing.

</details>


### [49] [CoDiR: Confidence-Guided Diffusion Refinement for Semi-Supervised Histopathology Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11807)
*Hoai Nhan Pham,Dang-Nguyen Bui,Le-Van Thai,Thanh-Hiep Vo,Lan Anh Dinh Thi,Tien Dat Nguyen,Duy-Dong Nguyen,Ngoc Lam Quang Bui,Tam Tran,Zhi Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CoDiR，一种结合Mean Teacher分割模型与扩散模型伪标签精炼的半监督组织病理图像分割框架。仅对低置信度区域进行扩散模型精炼，在GlaS和CRAG数据集上以10%-20%标注数据达到或超越最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半监督组织病理图像分割面临两大挑战：标注数据稀缺，以及腺体区域模糊导致不可靠的伪标签。

Method: 框架包含三个核心组件：1）Mean Teacher分割模型生成软预测；2）仅对低置信度区域用条件扩散模型进行精炼，该扩散模型从标注数据学习掩码结构；3）将精炼掩码与可靠教师预测融合，并通过置信度加权和一致性正则化训练学生模型。

Result: 在GlaS和CRAG数据集上，10%标注数据下达到88.09%和89.83%的mDice，20%标注数据下达到89.19%和90.29%的mDice，在八项基准指标中的七项上匹配或超越最强已有方法。消融实验表明精炼模块贡献最大，相比Mean Teacher基线提升+6.36% mDice。

Conclusion: CoDiR通过引入扩散模型进行低置信度区域的伪标签精炼，有效缓解了半监督组织病理分割中伪标签不可靠的问题，验证了扩散模型在半监督医学图像分割中的潜力。

Abstract: Semi-supervised histopathology segmentation is challenging due to scarce annotations and unreliable pseudo-labels in ambiguous gland regions. To address this problem, we propose Confidence-Guided Diffusion Refinement (CoDiR), a semi-supervised framework that combines a Mean Teacher segmentation model with diffusion-based pseudo-label refinement. Given an unlabeled image, the teacher first produces a soft prediction, and only low-confidence regions are refined by a conditional diffusion model trained to capture plausible mask structures from labeled data. The refined mask is then fused with reliable teacher predictions and used to train the student with confidence weighting and consistency regularization. On the GlaS and CRAG datasets CoDiR reaches 88.09\% and 89.83\% mDice with 10\% labeled data, and 89.19\% and 90.29\% mDice with 20\%, matching or exceeding the strongest published method on seven of the eight benchmark metrics. Ablations attribute the largest single contribution to the refinement module, which adds +6.36\% mDice over the Mean Teacher baseline. The implementation code is publicly available at: https://github.com/vongla345/codir

</details>


### [50] [Can Vision Models Read the Radar Display? On the Feasibility of Radar Imagery for Air Traffic Complexity Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11810)
*Hyewook Kim,Byul Kang,Seokbin Yoon,Keumjin Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文验证了雷达图像作为深度学习视觉模型输入以建模空中交通管制员所感知的复杂度的可行性。通过将交通态势编码为位置图像加五个状态变量通道（航向、速度、高度等），训练视觉Transformer（ViT）回归四种从飞机间几何关系推导的固有复杂度分量。模型在四个分量上均达到R²>0.96，且单机移除扰动实验显示模型响应与该飞机对扇区复杂度的实际贡献成比例变化，证明雷达图像虽与自然图像差异显著（稀疏、自相似），仍是可行的输入格式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中交通管制员主要通过雷达显示屏感知交通复杂度，因此基于相同图像的计算机视觉模型有望成为建模管制员感知复杂度的自然架构。但雷达图像与自然图像截然不同——极度稀疏、自相似，主要由黑色背景和少量视觉上相同的飞机光点构成，且飞机位置的微小变化会显著改变扇区级复杂度，因此需要验证雷达图像是否适合作为深度学习视觉模型的输入。

Method: 将每个交通态势编码为位置图像，并附加五个通道表示飞机状态变量（航向、速度、高度等）；采用Vision Transformer（ViT）架构，通过回归学习预测四种由飞机间两两几何关系推导出的固有复杂度分量。

Result: 模型在全部四个复杂度分量上取得R²>0.96的预测性能。单机移除扰动研究表明，模型对移除飞机后的响应变化与该飞机对扇区复杂度的实际贡献成比例，而非对所有移除操作等量响应。

Conclusion: 尽管雷达图像具有与非自然图像不同的典型视觉特征（极稀疏、高度自相似），它仍然是空中交通复杂度建模中一种可行的深度学习视觉模型输入格式。

Abstract: Air traffic controllers perceive traffic complexity through the radar display, suggesting that a computer vision model operating on the same imagery may provide a natural architecture for modeling controller-perceived complexity; however, whether radar imagery is a viable input format for deep learning vision models remains unclear. Unlike natural images, radar images are extremely sparse and self-similar, consisting primarily of a black background and a few visually identical aircraft blobs, while small changes in aircraft positions can substantially alter sector-level complexity. To test whether a vision model can capture these operationally important differences, we encode each traffic situation as a position image supplemented by five channels representing aircraft state variables, including heading, speed, and altitude, and train a Vision Transformer (ViT) to regress four intrinsic complexity components derived from pairwise geometric relations among aircraft. The model achieves $R^2 > 0.96$ for all four components, and a one-aircraft-removal perturbation study shows that its response changes proportionally to how much the removed aircraft contributed to sector complexity rather than treating every removal as equivalent. These results demonstrate that, despite its atypical visual characteristics, radar imagery is a viable input format for air traffic complexity modeling.

</details>


### [51] [TD-VAD: Breaking Visual Dependence in Video Anomaly Detection with Text-Driven Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11820)
*Shuangqing Zhang,Lei-Lei Ma,Zhao Wang,Wen Dong,Xinyi Xu,Guo-Sen Xie,Caifeng Shan,Fang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种文本驱动的视频异常检测方法（TD-VAD），利用LLM生成具有时间特征的文本描述来训练VAD模型，打破对视觉异常数据的依赖，在XD-Violence和UCF-Crime数据集上显著优于现有的单类和无监督VAD方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频异常检测（VAD）方法依赖大量标注的异常视觉数据进行训练，但异常数据稀缺且异常事件类型多样，获取充分标注数据困难且不可扩展。文本描述易于收集且类别标签可直接获取，因此作者提出用文本替代视觉数据来训练VAD模型。

Method: 1）利用LLM生成具有时间特征的"类视频"文本描述来训练VAD模型，完全无需目标域异常数据；2）设计事件演化因果注意力模块（Event Evolution Causal Attention Module）捕获事件跨时间的长短程时序逻辑依赖；3）推理阶段使用冻结的CLIP编码器提取视频帧嵌入以对齐文本模态，同时保留关键视觉信息。

Result: 在XD-Violence和UCF-Crime两个大规模VAD数据集上的综合实验表明，所提方法大幅优于先前的单类（one-class）和无监督VAD方法。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了将文本视为视频序列用于VAD模型的有效性，开辟了摆脱视觉数据依赖的VAD新范式，通过LLM生成文本描述和时序注意力建模实现了高性能的文本驱动视频异常检测。

Abstract: Visual data is typically a prerequisite for training existing video anomaly detection (VAD) methods. However, obtaining sufficient annotated anomaly data for training is challenging and not scalable due to the rarity of anomaly data and the wide variety of abnormal events. In this work, we advocate that the effectiveness of treating texts as video sequences for the VAD model and propose a novel Text-Driven Video Anomaly Detection (TD-VAD) approach to break visual dependence. In contrast to the anomaly video data, text descriptions of abnormal events are easy to collect, and their class labels can be directly derived. Specifically, our method utilizes video-like text descriptions with temporal characteristics generated by LLM to train a VAD model, without any reliance on target-domain anomaly data. To capture the long- and short-range temporal logic of events, we design the event evolution causal attention module to model contextual dependencies across time. During inference, considering the domain gap between the texts and video sequences, we use the frozen CLIP encoder to extract embeddings of video frames to align the text modality while retaining crucial visual information. Comprehensive experiments on two large-scale VAD datasets, XD-Violence and UCF-Crime, demonstrate that our method outperforms prior one-class and unsupervised VAD methods by a large margin.

</details>


### [52] [Distractor-Aware Video Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11835)
*Andreas Robinson,Abdelrahman Eldesokey,Michael Felsberg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种one-versus-many（一对多）的半监督视频目标分割方案，将干扰物（distractors）从背景中分离为独立类别，赋予其特殊注意力，在DAVIS 2017数据集上取得了新的最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的判别式方法通常将视频分割建模为前景与背景的一对一（one-versus-one）二分类问题。然而现实视频中通常包含目标、背景以及其他与目标视觉相似的干扰物，这些干扰物容易导致误报（false positives）。因此需要将干扰物与背景分离并独立处理。

Method: 提出one-versus-many（一对多）分类方案，将干扰物单独划分为一个类别，使模型能够对最可能导致性能退化的困难区域施加特殊注意力。具体实现上，对learning-what-to-learn（LWL）方法进行改造，使其具备干扰物感知能力（distractor-aware）。

Result: 在DAVIS 2017 val数据集上取得了新的最先进（state-of-the-art）结果；在DAVIS 2017 test-dev基准上较基线提升了4.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过将干扰物从背景中独立分离并作为单独类别处理，能够有效降低误报率从而提升半监督视频目标分割性能，验证了一对多分类方案的有效性。

Abstract: Semi-supervised video object segmentation is a challenging task that aims to segment a target throughout a video sequence given an initial mask at the first frame. Discriminative approaches have demonstrated competitive performance on this task at a sensible complexity. These approaches typically formulate the problem as a one-versus-one classification between the target and the background. However, in reality, a video sequence usually encompasses a target, background, and possibly other distracting objects. Those objects increase the risk of introducing false positives, especially if they share visual similarities with the target. Therefore, it is more effective to separate distractors from the background, and handle them independently.
  We propose a one-versus-many scheme to address this situation by separating distractors into their own class. This separation allows imposing special attention to challenging regions that are most likely to degrade the performance. We demonstrate the prominence of this formulation by modifying the learning-what-to-learn (LWL) method to be distractor-aware. Our proposed approach sets a new state-of-the-art on the DAVIS 2017 val dataset, and improves over the baseline on the DAVIS 2017 test-dev benchmark by 4.6 percentage points.

</details>


### [53] [GeoBridge: Decoupled Semantic Conditioning for Generative Image Geolocalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11838)
*Zhiyang Dou,Xumeng Han,Fengde Peng,Zipeng Wang,Moxuan Zhao,Zhipei Huang,Zhenjun Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoBridge提出了一种角色解耦的条件机制，将冻结的语义MLLM连接到冻结的黎曼流形匹配头，直接在球面上生成连续坐标，解决了语义监督与条件接口之间的角色冲突，在IM2GPS3K上达到38.67/52.89/70.37（25/200/750km阈值）的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在图像地理定位中主要改进的是对地理线索的推理能力，但推理结果如何解码为坐标的环节明显滞后。现有方法通过生成地名供地理编码API查询，这一过程是离散且有损的——它忽视了图像证据，并将多粒度语义压缩为粗糙的查表操作。作者认为瓶颈已从"模型推理什么"转移到"推理如何被表示为面向连续、几何感知解码器的输入"。

Method: GeoBridge采用角色解耦的条件化机制：将冻结的语义MLLM连接到冻结的黎曼流形匹配头（Riemannian flow-matching head）上，后者直接在球面上生成坐标。核心挑战是角色冲突：用离散语义标签监督条件会使其表征偏向类判别性的几何信息，与生成头所需的平滑流形相矛盾。GeoBridge将语义监督与条件接口解耦——通过一个独立的投影层构建生成头期望的连续条件，在不干扰球面解码器的情况下注入地理先验。

Result: 在IM2GPS3K基准上，GeoBridge在25/200/750km阈值下分别达到38.67/52.89/70.37的精度，在这些精度关键尺度上优于地名-API管线方法和推理增强的直接预测方法。

Conclusion: GeoBridge是一个解码端的算法贡献，与链式推理（chain-of-thought）方法正交且互补。它表明地理定位的瓶颈已从推理环节转移到推理表征与连续几何解码的衔接环节，并提供了一种与现有推理增强方法可叠加的解决方案。代码将公开。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have advanced image geolocalization mainly by improving how they reason about geographic cues. How that reasoning isdecoded into coordinates, however, has lagged behind. Predicting a place name for a geocoding API is discrete and lossy: it ignores image evidence and collapses multi-granular semantics into a coarse lookup. We argue that the bottleneck has shifted from what a model reasons to how that reasoning is represented for a continuous, geometry-aware decoder. We present GeoBridge, a role-decoupled conditioning mechanism that connects a frozen semantic MLLM to a frozen Riemannian flow-matching head that generates coordinates on the sphere. The central obstacle is arole conflict: supervising the condition with discrete semantic labels biases its representation toward class-discriminative geometry, at odds with the smooth manifold the generative head requires. GeoBridge keeps the semantic supervision decoupled from the condition interface: a separate projection forms the continuous condition the frozen head expects, injecting geographic priors without disturbing the spherical decoder. On IM2GPS3K, GeoBridge reaches 38.67/52.89/70.37 at the 25/200/750 km thresholds, improving over a place-name-to-API pipeline and reasoning-augmented direct prediction at these precision-relevant scales. GeoBridge is a decode-side algorithmic contribution, orthogonal and complementary to chain-of-thought reasoning. Code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [54] [BoltNet: An Ultra-Lightweight Convolutional Network for On-Device Plant Species Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11844)
*Daniel Rossi,Guido Borghi,Roberto Vezzani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BoltNet 是一个超轻量级全卷积架构，通过空间重分配瓶颈（Spatial Redistribution Bottleneck）和逻辑预采样（Logit PreSampling）技术在公民科学图像的高基数植物物种识别中实现了性能与模型大小之间的更优权衡，在 Pl@ntNet300K 上以 341K 参数（1.37 MB）达到 0.682 F1-score，并在 Raspberry Pi 5、Jetson Orin Nano 和 Hailo-8 三种硬件平台上展现了一致的执行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 植物物种自动识别是一个要求苛刻的细粒度识别问题，涉及大规模分类标签空间、视觉上相似的物种和长尾观测数据。模型大小只是部署成本的一部分——推理期间保存在内存中的中间激活值和平台相关的执行行为也同样重要，因此紧凑型识别模型必须在目标硬件上评估，而非仅依赖复杂度指标。

Method: 提出 BoltNet 超轻量级全卷积架构，结合了 Space Redistribution Bottleneck（空间重分配瓶颈）和 Logit PreSampling（逻辑预采样）两种新模块以改善高基数分类中预测性能与模型大小之间的权衡，并引入 AccuracyCompression Tradeoff 作为补充诊断指标。在 CPU、GPU 和 NPU 三种平台（Raspberry Pi 5、Jetson Orin Nano、Hailo-8）上进行模型级性能测量。

Result: 在 Pl@ntNet300K 上，BoltNet 以 341K 参数（1.37 MB）达到 0.682 F1-score，是评估中所有低于 2 MB 模型中 F1-score 最高的，接近显著更大的卷积骨干网络。在三种平台上 BoltNet 是最一致的效率模型，在 GPU 和 NPU 上取得最佳 FPS/W，在 CPU 上为第二最佳。AIDERv2 和 CLRS 上的结果提供了跨环境图像分类任务迁移的次要证据。

Conclusion: BoltNet 展示了在超轻量级模型约束下实现高精度识别是可行的，并强调了在实际部署场景中必须结合目标硬件执行效率来评估紧凑模型，而非仅考虑参数数量。该工作为嵌入式和高通量植物识别场景提供了实用的模型候选方案。

Abstract: Automated plant species identification from citizen-science imagery is an established, demanding fine-grained recognition problem: large taxonomic label spaces, visually similar species, and long-tailed observations require real model capacity, while field use constrains memory, latency, and power. Model size is only part of the deployment cost: intermediate activations held in memory during inference and platformdependent execution behavior matter too, so compact recognition must be assessed on target hardware rather than through complexity metrics alone. We present BoltNet, an ultra-lightweight fully convolutional architecture combining a Spatial Redistribution Bottleneck and Logit PreSampling to improve the tradeoff between predictive performance and model size in high-cardinality classification, and report the AccuracyCompression Tradeoff as a complementary diagnostic. On Pl@ntNet300K, BoltNet reaches 0.682 F1-score with 341K parameters (1.37 MB), the highest F1-score among evaluated models below 2 MB and close to substantially larger convolutional backbones. Model-only measurements on a Raspberry Pi 5, Jetson Orin Nano, and Hailo-8 characterize execution across CPU, GPU, and NPU platforms, where BoltNet is the most consistently efficient model, with the best FPS/W on the GPU and NPU and second-best on the CPU. Results on AIDERv2 and CLRS provide secondary evidence of transfer across environmental image-classification tasks. Code available at: https://codeberg.org/danielrossi/BoltNet

</details>


### [55] [LookBack: Where and How to Score LVLM Responses via Visual Reference Usage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11847)
*Beomsik Cho,Jinhyeong Kim,Dongseok Lee,Jaehyung Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LookBack，一种无需训练的LVLM响应评分方法，通过视觉回看分数增强token似然，用于衡量每个响应token对图像token的引用强度，在四个基准和三个模型上显著提升了Best-of-N选择的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLM不仅继承了文本层面的幻觉，还会产生与图像不符的幻觉。现有的基于置信度的评分指标（从LLM借鉴而来）不足以评估LVLM响应，因为移除输入图像后置信度选择几乎不变，说明输出空间的置信度主要捕捉文本合理性而非图像一致性。

Method: 提出LookBack方法，它是一种训练自由的LVLM响应评分方法，将token似然与视觉回看分数（visual lookback score）相结合。视觉回看分数是一种轻量级度量，用于衡量每个响应token对图像token的引用强度，从而确保响应与图像内容对齐。

Result: 在四个基准数据集和三个模型上，LookBack一致地提升了Best-of-N选择的表现，超过了现有基线方法，且额外计算开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: LookBack有效解决了LVLM响应评分的图像一致性缺口，通过引入视觉回看机制弥补了传统置信度指标对图像信息不敏感的不足，为LVLM响应选择提供了更可靠、高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) integrate visual perception with language generation, enabling responses that span image understanding and complex reasoning. However, LVLMs do not just inherit the text-level hallucinations; they also hallucinate against the image, producing fluent responses ungrounded in what they see. This makes LVLM response scoring inherently harder, and our diagnostics show that existing confidence-based metrics adopted from LLMs are insufficient for LVLMs. Specifically, removing the input image barely changes confidence-based selection, suggesting that output-space confidence primarily captures textual plausibility rather than agreement with the image. To address this gap, we propose LookBack, a training-free LVLM response scoring method that augments token likelihood with visual lookback score, a lightweight measure of how strongly each response token refers to image tokens. Across four benchmarks and three models, LookBack consistently improves Best-of-$N$ selection over existing baselines with negligible additional overhead.

</details>


### [56] [Warping Earth Observations for better ice labeling in the Marginal Marginal Ice Zone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11883)
*Tom Kelly,Martin S. J. Rogers*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于互信息（mutual information）变换对齐的多模态卫星图像配准架构，解决南极边缘冰区中不同传感器（Sentinel-1和MODIS）因表面特征漂移导致的像素级不对齐问题。作者引入一个包含2,088个像素级标注和7,046个专家点分类的稀疏专家标注数据集（覆盖43个场景的冰水边缘界面），证明在分割前进行空间配准和对齐多模态数据可提升分类精度，并支持从稀疏点级监督实现密集海冰分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态卫星图像为对地观测提供互补信息，但动态环境中异质传感器的精确融合面临挑战。南极边缘冰区中冰面特征在多次图像采集之间发生移动，导致空间和时间上的不匹配，破坏了大多数多模态推理和分类流程所依赖的像素级对应假设。此外，海冰密集监督标签稀缺，像素级标注需要专家对噪声数据进行耗时的判读，历史上只能依赖粗分辨率海事冰图进行模型训练。

Method: 本文提出一种基于互信息变换（mutual information warping）的新型架构，用于对齐多卫星（Sentinel-1和MODIS）、多模态（可见光、热红外、雷达）卫星场景。具体包括：1) 构建一个稀疏专家标注数据集，包含2,088个像素级标注和7,046个专家点分类，位于43个场景的冰水边缘界面；2) 在分割之前对多模态数据进行空间grounding和对齐；3) 利用对齐后的数据实现从稀疏点级监督到密集海冰分割的转化。

Result: 实验结果表明，在分割前对模态进行空间grounding和对齐能够显著提高分类精度，并且能够仅从稀疏的点级监督中实现准确、密集的海冰分割。

Conclusion: 空间配准和对齐是多模态地观测图像分析的关键前置步骤，尤其对于动态场景（如南极海冰区域）。通过互信息变换对齐可有效缓解跨传感器时空失配问题，并使得在标注资源稀缺条件下仍能实现高质量密集分割成为可能。

Abstract: Multimodal satellite imagery provides complementary information for Earth Observation, but accurately combining heterogeneous sensors remains challenging in dynamic environments. Fast-changing regions, such as the Antarctic marginal ice zone, cannot fully exploit multimodal information from different satellite sensors because surface features move between image acquisitions. This spatial and temporal mismatch challenges effective perceptual grounding, violating the assumption of pixel-level correspondence that underpins most multimodal reasoning and downstream classification pipelines. Antarctic sea ice provides a challenging benchmark due to the rapid, heterogeneous drift of individual ice floes and the differing responses of sea ice to radar, visible and thermal sensing modalities. Accurate, dense supervision of sea ice remains scarce because generating pixel-wise labels requires time-consuming expert interpretation of noisy data, leading to historical reliance on coarse-resolution maritime ice charts for model training. This paper presents a novel architecture based on mutual information warping to align multi-satellite (Sentinel-1 and MODIS platforms) multimodal (visible, thermal, radar) satellite scenes. To demonstrate the approach, we introduce a sparse expert-labeled dataset of 2,088 pixel-wise annotations (7,046 expert point classifications) located at the ice-water margin interface across 43 scenes. Our results demonstrate that spatially grounding and aligning modalities prior to segmentation improves classification accuracy, and enables accurate, dense sea ice segmentation from sparse point-wise supervision.

</details>


### [57] [Do You See What You Draw? A Semantic Closed-Loop Framework for Holistic Evaluation of Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11907)
*Hao Zhang,Jiaxin Qi,Zhijiang Tang,Jianqiang Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Self-Generative-Understanding (SGU)，一个无需标注的统一多模态模型(UMM)评估框架，通过"感知图像→生成文本描述→重建视觉上下文→自我推理"的语义闭环管道，为统一多模态模型提供集成性能评分，揭示其感知与生成能力之间的潜在断裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估协议将多模态模型的生成能力和判别能力分开评估，缺乏针对统一多模态模型(UMM)的系统级、一体化评估方案，导致模型在自身生成内容上推理的缺陷无法被捕获。

Method: SGU框架通过四个步骤构成语义闭环：模型先理解输入图像并生成文本描述，随后基于该描述重建视觉上下文，最后对自生成输出进行推理。整个过程无需任何人工标注，零成本构建测试平台，并输出统一的集成性能评分。

Result: 实验表明，即使是高性能的UMMs也常常无法对自身生成的上下文进行有效推理，这种缺陷在单独评估理解或生成能力时不会被发现，揭示了统一模型在感知与生成跨模态闭环中的系统性局限。

Conclusion: SGU提供了一个互补的整体性评估框架，为下一代统一多模态模型的基准测试奠定了基础，有助于推动统一模型在感知与生成能力上的协同发展。

Abstract: As Large Vision-Language Models increasingly aim to integrate visual generation and understanding within a single parameter space, evaluating such structural unification in a cohesive manner remains a critical challenge. Current evaluation protocols predominantly treat generative and discriminative capabilities as separate tasks, leaving a gap in system-level evaluation for unified multimodal models (UMMs). In this work, we propose Self-Generative-Understanding (SGU), a novel, annotation-free evaluation framework that probes the integrated capabilities of unified models through a semantic closed-loop challenge. Without requiring new annotations, SGU leverages the dual understanding-and-generation abilities of UMMs by asking them to first perceive an image and produce a textual description, subsequently reconstruct a visual context based on that description, and finally perform reasoning over the self-generated output. This pipeline provides a zero-cost testbed that yields an integrated performance score specifically tailored for evaluating UMMs as unified systems. Extensive experiments show that even high-performing UMMs often struggle to reason over their own generated contexts, revealing limitations that are not captured by separate evaluations of understanding or generation alone. Our work provides a complementary holistic evaluation framework and offers a foundation for benchmarking the development of next-generation unified multimodal models.

</details>


### [58] [HarmoniDPO: Video-guided Audio Generation via Preference-Optimized Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11913)
*Wenshuo Peng,Kaipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HarmoniDPO框架，通过双视频表征（全局上下文+逐帧特征）保留时间动态与语义细节，结合在线直接偏好优化（online-DPO）微调扩散式视频转音频（V2A）模型，并引入双尺度扩散搜索（DDS）作为测试时缩放算法，在音视频同步和主观音频质量上超越现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有V2A方法将视频压缩为单一特征表示，导致时间动态和细粒度视觉信息显著丢失；同时依赖重建式训练目标，与人类对音频质量和匹配度的感知判断相关性差。

Method: (1) 提出双视频表征策略，结合全局上下文与逐帧特征以保留时间动态和语义信息；(2) 受RLHF启发，采用在线直接偏好优化（online-DPO）基于偏好判断微调扩散式V2A模型，提升感知质量与对齐度；(3) 提出双尺度扩散搜索（DDS）测试时缩放算法，在推理阶段自适应优化输出保真度。

Result: 实验表明HarmoniDPO在音视频同步性和主观音频质量上均优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: HarmoniDPO为从视频生成真实且受人类偏好的音频提供了稳健的解决方案，验证了偏好优化与多样征融合在V2A任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Video-to-audio (V2A) generation faces significant challenges in achieving precise temporal synchronization and high perceptual quality due to the complex, ambiguous relationship between visual and auditory cues. Existing methods typically compress video inputs into single feature representations, leading to significant loss of temporal dynamics and fine-grained visual information. These approaches also rely on reconstruction-based training objectives that poorly correlate with human perceptual judgments of audio quality and appropriateness. We propose HarmoniDPO, a novel framework that integrates preference-based optimization into diffusion-based V2A generation to address these limitations. (1) Our approach leverages a dual video representation: combining global context with frame-wise features to preserve temporal dynamics and semantic detail. (2) Inspired by reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF), HarmoniDPO employs online Direct Preference Optimization (online-DPO) to fine-tune a diffusion-based V2A model from preference judgments, enhancing perceptual quality and alignment. (3) Additionally, we introduce Dual-scale Diffusion Search (DDS), a test time scaling algorithm that adaptively optimizes output fidelity during inference. Experiments demonstrate that HarmoniDPO outperforms state-of-the-art methods in audio-video synchronization and subjective audio quality, offering a robust solution for generating realistic, human-preferred audio from video.

</details>


### [59] [Seed2GS: Camera-Free, Training-Free Object Extraction from 3D Gaussian Scenes via a Single Reference-View Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11928)
*Zongjian Ding,Yudong Gao,Jiale Liu,Xinglin Yu,Junxing Ren,Dong Wei,Yajing Chen,Shan Huang,Mingjun Cheng,Min Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Seed2GS是一种无需原始重建相机、无需场景特定表示训练的3DGS目标提取方法，通过QD-SAM3选择可靠参考掩膜固定目标身份，再通过种子提升与可见性自适应虚拟轨道传播掩膜，仅训练临时前景logit，在LERF-MASK上达到92.1% mIoU（9.3秒延迟），优于所有现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从预构建的3DGS场景中提取目标物体对交互式3D编辑至关重要。现有方法要么每个场景需训练数十分钟，要么牺牲准确性，要么依赖预构建资产可能不包含的原始重建相机，限制了实际应用。

Method: 核心思想是将目标身份与3D覆盖范围分离。具体包括：(1) 使用QD-SAM3从多个开放词汇候选掩膜中选择一个可靠的参考掩膜以固定目标身份；(2) 通过种子提升和可见性自适应虚拟轨道从新视角暴露物体；(3) 利用跟踪传播种子而无需重复检测；(4) 场景保持冻结，掩膜仅监督每个高斯的单个临时前景logit。

Result: 在LERF-MASK上达到92.1% mIoU，计算延迟仅9.3秒，比最强的场景训练基线高3.7个点，比最近的免相机基线高7.6个点。使用固定测试参考时达到91.1% mIoU；用真值掩膜替代预测种子仅提升0.72个点。在3D-OVS上达到95.7% mIoU。

Conclusion: Seed2GS在无原始相机和免场景训练的条件下实现了迄今最高的LERF-MASK精度，验证了将目标身份与3D覆盖分离的有效性，为预构建3DGS资产的交互式编辑提供了高效精确的解决方案。

Abstract: Extracting a target object from a pre-built 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) scene enables interactive 3D editing. Existing methods either train for tens of minutes per scene, sacrifice accuracy, or require original reconstruction cameras that pre-built assets may not include. We present Seed2GS, which achieves the highest reported LERF-MASK accuracy without original reconstruction cameras or scene-specific representation training. Its key insight is to separate target identity from 3D coverage. QD-SAM3 selects one reliable reference mask from several open-vocabulary candidates, fixing identity once. Seed lift and visibility-adaptive virtual orbits then expose the object from new viewpoints, while tracking propagates the seed without repeated detection. Because the scene remains frozen, these masks supervise only one temporary foreground logit per Gaussian. On LERF-MASK, Seed2GS reaches 92.1% mean intersection over union (mIoU) with a measured compute-only latency of 9.3 seconds, 3.7 points above the strongest scene-trained baseline and 7.6 points above the closest camera-free baseline. With one fixed test reference per scene, the complete pipeline retains 91.1% mIoU; replacing its predicted seed with a ground-truth mask improves mIoU by only 0.72 points. On 3D-OVS, Seed2GS reaches 95.7% mIoU.

</details>


### [60] [Dual Anchors, Do It Better: Hierarchical Group Merging for Zero-Shot Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11933)
*Jimin Roh,DongKyu Kim,Suk-Ju Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种双锚定（Dual-Anchor）框架，通过层级图像锚定与文本锚定互补，使用自上而下的分组机制构建图像锚点，并通过Group-Gated Token Refiner增强全局表征，在8个工业和6个医学基准上实现更强的零样本异常检测泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CLIP-based零样本异常检测方法仅依赖文本模态锚定语义，导致对提示词设计高度敏感且视觉接地能力弱，难以应对工业与医学等频繁发生域偏移的场景。

Method: 提出Dual-Anchor框架：(1)通过自上而下的分组机制构建层级图像锚点，逐步聚合局部到全局的图像特征形成正常与异常组令牌；(2)利用Group-Gated Token Refiner中组令牌作为门控信号增强全局表征；(3)将精炼后的图像锚点与文本提示融合，构建动态状态提示，联合强化视觉与文本语义。

Result: 在8个工业基准和6个医学基准上展现出强大的泛化能力，稳定了图像-文本对齐并降低了对提示词的依赖。

Conclusion: 双锚定策略通过引入层级图像锚点有效弥补了单一文本锚定的不足，提升了图像-文本对齐的稳定性并减少了提示词敏感性问题，为跨域零样本异常检测提供了更鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Zero-shot anomaly detection (ZSAD) aims to identify anomalies in unseen domains, a setting that is particularly critical for industrial and medical applications where domain shifts are prevalent. However, most CLIP-based ZSAD methods anchor semantics solely on the text modality, making performance highly sensitive to prompt design and leading to weak visual grounding. To mitigate these limitations, we propose a Dual-Anchor framework that complements conventional text anchors with hierarchical image anchors constructed via a top-down grouping mechanism. This mechanism progressively aggregates local-to-global image features to form normal and abnormal group tokens, which serve as image anchors and act as gating signals in a Group-Gated Token Refiner to enhance the global representation. The refined image anchors are then fused with text prompts to construct dynamic state prompts. By jointly reinforcing visual and textual semantics, our framework stabilizes image-text alignment, reduces prompt dependency, and achieves strong generalization across 8 industrial and 6 medical benchmarks.

</details>


### [61] [Surfsvr: 2D Surface Priors as 3D Geometric Regularizers for Sparse Voxel Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11938)
*Yan Di,Chengxi Li,Yaoxing Wang,Mengge Liu,Zhigang Li,Ruida Zhang,Mingyang Li,Pengyuan Wang,Shan Gao,Xiangyang Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SurfSVR，一种新的稀疏体素重建范式，将2D表面先验作为显式的3D几何正则化器，通过将图像组织为连贯的表面区域并提升为3D结构约束，显著改善稀疏体素重建质量。在3个公开基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的稀疏体素重建通常从局部光度证据和离散可见性统计中优化几何，导致表面碎片化、过度细分以及浮动伪影，尤其在弱纹理或稀疏观测区域。论文旨在通过引入2D表面先验作为显式3D几何正则化器来系统性地解决这一问题。

Method: SurfSVR首先通过联合推理外观、单目深度、法向及跨视图几何，将每张图像组织为连贯的表面区域；每个区域基于拟合可靠性和几何复杂度自适应地选择平面或二次曲面模型，并通过跨模型一致性区分可靠几何与模糊预测。这些结构化的2D先验被提升到3D并贯穿整个重建流程：引导表面自适应体素细分、提供区域级深度与法向监督、增强稀疏观测表面的体素剪枝、并在后精炼训练中抑制离表面浮动伪影。

Result: 在3个公开基准上进行了大量实验，SurfSVR在具有显著不同可见性和几何特征的场景中一致地提升了稀疏体素重建质量，达到当前最优的重建效果。

Conclusion: SurfSVR通过将图像空间中的语义和几何连贯性转化为3D中持续的结构约束，实现了统一且高效的设计，验证了将2D表面先验作为显式3D正则化器在稀疏体素重建中的有效性。

Abstract: Sparse voxel reconstruction offers an efficient representation for high-fidelity 3D modeling, yet its geometry is commonly optimized from local photometric evidence and discrete visibility statistics. This often leads to fragmented surfaces, excessive subdivision, and floating artifacts, particularly in weakly textured or sparsely observed regions. We introduce SurfSVR, a novel sparse voxel reconstruction paradigm that treats 2D surface priors as explicit 3D geometric regularizers. Instead of directly lifting noisy pixel-wise depth predictions, SurfSVR first organizes each image into coherent surface regions by jointly reasoning over appearance, monocular depth, normals and cross-view geometry. Each region is then represented by an adaptively selected planar or quadratic surface model based on fitting reliability and geometric complexity, while cross-model agreement distinguishes reliable geometry from ambiguous predictions. These structured 2D priors are lifted into 3D and integrated throughout the reconstruction pipeline. They guide surface-adaptive voxel subdivision, provide region-level depth and normal supervision during optimization, enhance geometrically reliable sparse-observed surfaces in voxel pruning, and suppress off-surface floaters during post-refinement training. This unified design converts semantic and geometric coherence in image space into persistent structural constraints in 3D. Extensive experiments on 3 public benchmarks demonstrate that SurfSVR consistently improves sparse voxel reconstruction across scenes with substantially different visibility and geometry characteristics, achieving state-of-the-art reconstruction quality. Codes and models will be released soon.

</details>


### [62] [Evaluating and Calibrating Diffusion Model-derived Uncertainty for Quantitative MRI Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11942)
*Shishuai Wang,Stefan Klein,Juan A. Hernandez-Tamames,Dirk H. J. Poot*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了基于扩散模型的数据一致qMRI框架中多次推断导出的不确定性图，发现其与映射误差正相关，可用于选择性预测，但原始不确定性校准不佳，需事后校准才能用于定量区间解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定量MRI可提供标准化的组织参数图，但基于深度学习的qMRI映射方法的可靠性往往未被明确表征。因此需要系统评估深度学习qMRI方法产生的不确定性图的有效性与校准性。

Method: 基于数据一致扩散模型的qMRI框架，通过多次推断生成不确定性图；在合成测试数据上评估误差感知性、高误差检测、选择性预测和高斯区间校准；采用结合预测值依赖的偏差校正与标量不确定性缩放的事后校准流程改进校准性能；在健康志愿者上做定性评估。

Result: 扩散模型导出的不确定性与映射误差正相关；风险覆盖分析显示排除高不确定性体素可降低保留误差；但原始不确定性对定量区间解释校准不佳，事后校准可显著改善校准性能；健康志愿者上观察到空间上有意义的不确定性模式。

Conclusion: 扩散模型导出的不确定性对可靠性评估和选择性预测具有信息价值，但需要经过校准才能用于定量区间解释。

Abstract: Quantitative MRI (qMRI) provides standardised tissue parameter maps, but the reliability of deep learning-based qMRI mapping methods is often not explicitly characterised. In this work we systematically evaluate uncertainty maps for quantitative MRI derived from multiple inferences of a data-consistent diffusion model-based qMRI framework. Evaluation on synthetic test data assessed error-awareness, high-error detection, selective prediction, and Gaussian interval calibration. Diffusion model-derived uncertainty was positively associated with the mapping error, while risk-coverage analysis showed that excluding high-uncertainty voxels reduced the retained error. However, the raw uncertainty was poorly calibrated for quantitative interval interpretation. Calibration was substantially improved using a post-hoc procedure combining prediction-value-dependent bias correction with scalar uncertainty scaling. Qualitative evaluation on a healthy volunteer showed spatially meaningful uncertainty patterns. These results indicate that diffusion model-derived uncertainty is informative for reliability assessment and selective prediction, but requires calibration for quantitative interval interpretation.

</details>


### [63] [Auditing Frame-Level AUC in Weakly Supervised Video Anomaly Detection: Granularity, Resolution, and Scene Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11985)
*Sara Abdulaziz,Egor Bondarev*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对弱监督视频异常检测（WSVAD）中的帧级AUC评估指标进行了系统性审计，发现现有的池化AUC（跨视频聚合帧对）不能可靠反映视频内异常定位能力，在基准测试规模下缺乏分辨率以支持SOTA模型之间的微小差距，且模型在正常视频上会依据拍摄属性（如分辨率、色彩编码）分离视频，表明场景敏感性是共享的而非架构特异的。作者公开了一个可感知粒度的评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前WSVAD领域以帧级AUC作为主流评估指标，但其标准形式（池化AUC）将来自不同视频的帧对聚合比较，既衡量了事件定位能力，也混杂了视频来源间的差异。这种混杂可能掩盖模型的真实定位性能差异，导致排名不可靠。本文旨在审计该协议的有效性，检验池化AUC在多大程度上能够可靠反映WSVAD的核心目标——视频内异常定位。

Method: 在UCF-Crime基准上，对跨不同骨干网络家族的多个SOTA模型进行评估审计。方法包括：(1) 固定每个模型的帧分数，在三种配对粒度（全局、按异常类别、视频内）下重新读取模型性能；(2) 在三种粒度下对由模型内部表示计算的零样本分数进行相同的读取；(3) 使用配对视频bootstrap评估排名可靠性；(4) 在正常视频上验证模型是否按记录属性分离。

Result: 三个主要发现：(1) 池化AUC不能可靠预测视频内异常定位——池化分数相似的模型在更严格粒度下存在大的定位差异和排名反转；(2) 在测试集规模下，池化AUC缺乏分辨率以支持领域报告的SOTA差距——在每类骨干家族内无法区分这些微小差异，而视频内AUC在相同预测上能区分多个比较；(3) 所有模型在正常视频上都能按视频记录属性（如分辨率、色彩编码）分离视频，表明场景敏感性是该设定下的共享特征而非架构特异。此外，学习表示显示视频内异常结构与检测器定位是解耦的。

Conclusion: 池化AUC作为WSVAD的主导指标存在系统性缺陷：它混淆了视频来源差异与事件定位能力，缺乏对SOTA之间微小差距的区分分辨率，且模型普遍对场景属性敏感。作者主张采用粒度感知的评估协议（如视频内AUC），并公开了可复用的协议工具以推动该领域更可靠的评估标准。

Abstract: Frame-level area under the ROC curve (AUC) is the dominant evaluation metric for weakly supervised video anomaly detection (WSVAD). Its standard form measures whether an anomalous frame outranks a normal frame drawn from anywhere in the test set. We refer to this comparison as pooled AUC, since it aggregates frame pairs across test videos regardless of source. Pooled AUC therefore credits both event localization and differences between video sources. We audit this protocol on UCF-Crime across recent state-of-the-art models spanning different backbone families. Holding each model's frame scores fixed, we read them under three pairing granularities: global, per anomaly category, and within each video, then repeat the same three-granularity readout on zero-shot scores computed from the models' internal representations. We assess ranking reliability with a paired video bootstrap. Three findings follow. First, pooled AUC does not reliably predict within-video anomaly localization: models with similar pooled scores exhibit large localization differences and rank reversals under stricter granularities. Second, at the benchmark's test-split size, pooled AUC lacks the resolution to support state-of-the-art margins reported in the field. Within each backbone family, it resolves no comparison at those margins, while within-video AUC resolves several over identical predictions. Learned representations further reveal that within-video anomaly structure and detector localization are decoupled. Third, on normal footage alone, every model we examine separates videos by recording properties, such as resolution and color encoding, indicating that scene sensitivity is shared across the setting rather than specific to any architecture. We publicly release a granularity-aware protocol computable from existing predictions and scene-factor annotations for UCF-Crime.

</details>


### [64] [A Remote Approach to Cashew Orchard Detection: Leveraging Active Learning with Satellite Imagery in Guinea-Bissau](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11996)
*Miguel,Sofia,Maria,Patrícia,Luke,João*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究使用基于Sentinel-2卫星影像和机器学习技术，结合基于边际的主动学习方法，在几内亚比绍全国范围内实现了腰果果园的自动检测，绘制了10米分辨率的2021年腰果地图，平衡精度达94.0%，并公开了数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腰果生产是几内亚比绍及西非其他国家广泛的经济活动，但无监管的腰果种植与地区性森林砍伐、生物多样性丧失和经济结构脆弱直接相关。目前缺乏全国范围的腰果果园数据库，亟需通过遥感手段在大范围尺度上绘制腰果果园位置。

Method: 基于Sentinel-2卫星影像，采用机器学习技术实现腰果果园的自动检测。利用基于边际的主动学习（Margin-based Active Learning）技术优化训练集，以最少的标注点数实现最高的信息量，从而在全国范围内构建训练数据并完成腰果地图绘制。

Result: 腰果地图的平衡精度达到94.0%，且完全在地外（off-site）条件下获得；生成了两个数据集及2021年10米空间分辨率的全国腰果地图，并已通过GitHub公开共享。

Conclusion: 研究证明了将腰果果园遥感绘图从区域尺度扩展到全国尺度的可行性，为这一环境应用提供了新的基础，推动了更大范围的腰果种植监测与可持续管理。

Abstract: Cashew production is a widespread economic activity in Guinea-Bissau, as well as other countries in West Africa. However, unregulated cashew production can be directly associated with increasing regionwide deforestation rates, biodiversity losses, and a fragile economic structure. There is no nationwide database for listing or georeferencing cashew orchards, so there is a clear need to remotely map their locations. In recent years, multiple methods for detecting orchards have been developed, though they have only been applied on a regional level. This work expands regional analyses to a nationwide scale. It develops a scalable and cost-effective remote approach, based on Sentinel-2 satellite imagery, using Machine Learning techniques to detect cashew orchards automatically. Margin-based Active Learning techniques were employed to develop an optimal training set in terms of the number of points and their informativeness, leading to a cashew map with 94.0% balanced accuracy obtained entirely off-site. We created two datasets and a 2021 cashew map with 10m spatial resolution that are openly accessible through GitHub. The results demonstrate the possibility of a broader cashew orchard mapping, creating a new stepping stone for this environmental application.

</details>


### [65] [LoSA: Near-Lossless Sparse Attention for Training-Free Video Diffusion Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12032)
*Enhuai Liu,Yunke Wang,Yutong Wang,Changming Sun,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LoSA是一种训练无关的稀疏注意力方法，通过固定99%的注意力质量保留阈值而非稀疏比率，在视频扩散Transformer中实现了更好的速度-质量权衡。

实现细节：在早期一个稠密步骤测量精确的块注意力质量，为每个头和查询块保留满足阈值的最小键/值块集合，并在后续所有步骤中复用这些冻结的块索引。在Wan2.1-1.3B上单独获得1.36倍加速（VBench下降0.06点）；与特征缓存组合后在HunyuanVideo上达到3.2倍加速（仅下降0.02点），优于最强稀疏基线0.32点的下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散Transformer的采样成本高昂：每个去噪步骤都需要对长3D token序列进行自注意力计算，其二次复杂度随着分辨率和时长增长而急剧增加。现有稀疏注意力方法追求激进稀疏化，导致进一步加速时注意力保真度损失不成比例地增大。作者转向该权衡的另一端：通过构造保证近无损的保真度，在此约束下尽可能多地移除计算。两个关键观察支撑了这一方法：约40%的块间交互可移除而保留99%的注意力质量，且高质量支撑集在去噪步骤间保持稳定。

Method: LoSA通过固定保留质量阈值（99%）而非稀疏比率来设计稀疏注意力。具体步骤：1) 在早期一个稠密步骤测量精确的块级注意力质量；2) 为每个头和每个查询块，选择满足99%质量阈值的最小键/值块集合；3) 在剩余所有去噪步骤中复用冻结的块索引，避免重复计算。

Result: 在Wan2.1-1.3B上，LoSA单独获得1.36倍加速，VBench Overall仅下降0.06点。与特征缓存组合后，在HunyuanVideo上达到3.2倍加速，质量仅下降0.02点，而同速下最强稀疏基线下降0.32点。在三个视频扩散Transformer和最高3.2倍加速下，LoSA一致实现了最佳的训练无关速度-质量权衡。

Conclusion: LoSA证明了"先保证保真度再最大化加速"的范式可行：通过在早期稠密步骤测量块注意力质量并跨步骤复用索引，可以在保持近无损质量的同时显著降低视频扩散Transformer的采样成本。该方法的优势在与其他加速技术（如特征缓存）组合时最为显著，为训练无关的视频生成加速提供了一种高质量的基准方案。

Abstract: Video diffusion transformers are costly to sample: every denoising step applies self-attention over a long 3D token sequence, a quadratic cost that dominates as resolution and duration grow. Sparse attention reduces this cost without retraining, but existing methods pursue aggressive sparsity, where further speedup costs disproportionately more attention fidelity. We target the opposite end of this trade-off: fix near-lossless fidelity by construction, and remove as much computation as this constraint permits. Two observations make this regime practical: roughly 40% of block interactions can be removed while retaining 99% of the attention mass, and the high-mass support remains stable across denoising steps. We propose LoSA, a training-free sparse-attention method that fixes a retained-mass threshold of 99% rather than a sparsity ratio: it measures exact block attention masses at one early dense step, keeps, for each head and query block, the smallest key/value block set meeting the threshold, and reuses the frozen block indices for all remaining steps. On Wan2.1-1.3B, LoSA alone gives a $1.36\times$ speedup with a 0.06-point VBench Overall drop. The benefit is largest under composition: combined with feature caching, LoSA reaches a $3.2\times$ speedup on HunyuanVideo at a 0.02-point drop, versus 0.32 points for the strongest sparse baseline at comparable speed. Across three video diffusion transformers and speedups up to $3.2\times$, LoSA consistently achieves the best training-free speed-quality trade-off.

</details>


### [66] [How Far from Clinical Deployment? Evaluating the Complete Unsupervised Domain Adaptation Pipeline in Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12035)
*Yiheng Xiong,Luisa Gallée,Daniel Santak Wolf,Heiko Hillenhagen,Michael Götz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了无监督域适应（UDA）在临床实践中的完整流水线，包括适应算法和免标签的模型选择方法。在11个临床相关的跨域场景、9个医学影像数据集、10种UDA算法和13种验证器中评估了超过80,000个训练模型，发现虽然通常存在性能优秀的适应模型，但无标签条件下难以选出它。集成和少量目标标注预算可以缩小但无法完全消除与最优模型之间的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署UDA到临床实践中时，需要选择使用哪种算法以及部署其哪个训练模型。但目标域没有标签，无法直接评估模型，导致模型选择成为难题。此前的UDA研究多关注适应算法本身，而忽视了与算法同等重要的免标签模型选择问题，作者希望从完整流水线的视角评估并解决这一问题。

Method: 作者对UDA全流水线（适应+免标签选择）进行大规模系统评估：覆盖9个医学影像数据集的11个临床跨域场景，使用10种UDA算法和13种选择方法（验证器），总计评估超过80,000个训练模型。此外探索了两种缩小验证器选择与最优模型性能差距的策略：集成（ensembling）和少量目标标注预算。

Result: 发现：（1）通常存在性能优秀的适应模型，但在无目标标签条件下难以识别；（2）验证器选择的模型与最佳可用模型之间存在较大的结构化目标性能差距，且没有一种验证器在所有场景中持续可靠；（3）集成和少量目标标注两种策略均能缩小但无法完全消除该差距。

Conclusion: 可部署的UDA取决于完整流水线（适应+选择）的效果。选择步骤是当前UDA研究中较少被探索的关键环节，若能改善该步骤，将推动当前UDA方法向临床应用大幅迈进。

Abstract: Deploying unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA) in clinical practice requires choosing which algorithm to use and which of its trained models to ship. However, the deployment (target) domain is unlabeled, so models cannot be evaluated directly on it, leaving it unclear which to select. We address this by evaluating the complete UDA pipeline, considering both adaptation and label-free selection together.
