We further identify a perplexity-accuracy disconnect and trace it to where quantization concentrates damage: early-depth errors (Llama) propagate downstream and resist local correction, while late-depth errors (Qwen) do not. A layer-restricted variant of LCD validates this mechanism directly.

</details>


### [133] [GRPO for Financial Advice Generation: Outperforming Commercial LLMs under CATE Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11787)
*Ofir Ben Shoham,Shrutendra Harsola,Vignesh Subrahmaniam,Shravan Mohan,Yakov Gazman,Oded Vainas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将财务建议生成建模为强化学习问题，使用GRPO微调开源语言模型，并以LLM作为裁判的奖励函数结合安全门控进行优化；同时引入基于双重稳健CATE估计量的法官无关审计来验证真实商业价值。训练后的模型在估计毛利提升上约为最强商业基线的两倍（0.0228 vs 0.0104），且下行风险最低；值得注意的是，法官评估与因果审计对基线的排名不一致，表明因果审计捕捉到了法官无法辨识的信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从商业记录中生成可执行的财务建议需要模型整合数值推理、领域知识和合理判断，同时避免有害建议。直接监督训练存在困难：历史决策不一定最优，高质量的自由文本标签获取成本高昂。此外，仅依赖LLM-as-a-judge评估无法确认改进是否反映真实商业价值而非对裁判的过拟合，因此需要法官无关的验证手段。

Method: 将财务建议生成形式化为强化学习问题，使用Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)对开源权重语言模型进行微调。奖励函数采用LLM-as-a-judge评分规则，该规则在多个二元质量维度上对每条建议打分，并配备安全门控以防止有害建议。同时设计了一个基于标准双重稳健条件平均处理效应（CATE）估计器的观测性离策略审计，作为独立于LLM裁判的评估方法。

Result: 训练后的LLM在估计毛利提升上约为最强商业基线的两倍（0.0228 vs 0.0104），同时具有最低的下行率和最小的负尾风险。值得注意的是，两种评估对基线排名不一致：未训练的基座模型在裁判评分中排名最末但在因果审计中排名第二，表明审计捕捉到了裁判未捕获的信号。

Conclusion: GRPO结合财务导向的奖励信号能够产生比商业LLM更有用的商业建议；法官独立的因果审计是对LLM-as-a-judge评估的有价值补充而非确认，特别是在金融NLP领域。

Abstract: Generating actionable financial advice from business records demands that models integrate numerical reasoning, domain knowledge, and sound judgment, while avoiding recommendations that could harm the business. Direct supervision is difficult: historical decisions are not necessarily optimal, and high-quality free-form labels are expensive to obtain. We formulate financial advice generation as a reinforcement learning problem and fine-tune an open-weight language model using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). Our reward is an LLM-as-a-judge rubric that scores each recommendation across multiple binary dimensions of advice quality, augmented with a safety gate for harm prevention.
  Since LLM-based evaluation alone cannot confirm whether improvements reflect genuine business value rather than adaptation to the judge, we complement it with a judge-independent audit based on a standard doubly-robust Conditional Average Treatment Effect (CATE) estimator. Under this observational off-policy audit, our trained LLM achieves approximately twice the estimated gross-profit lift of the strongest evaluated commercial baseline ($0.0228$ vs.\ $0.0104$), together with the lowest downside rate and the least negative tail risk of any policy evaluated. Notably, the two evaluations do not rank the baselines identically: the untrained base model places last on the judge rubric but second on the causal audit, indicating that the audit captures a signal the judge does not. Our results demonstrate that GRPO with a finance-grounded reward signal can produce substantially more useful business recommendations than commercial LLMs, and that a judge-independent causal audit is a valuable complement to, rather than a confirmation of, LLM-as-a-judge assessment in financial NLP.

</details>


### [134] [TELLME: Test-Enhanced Learning for Language Model Enrichment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11788)
*Minjun Kim,Inho Won,Hyeonseok Lim,MinKyu Kim,Junghun Yuk,Wooyoung Go,Jongyoul Park,Jungyeul Park,KyungTae Lim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出TELLME方法，通过测试增强学习（TEL）原理结合持续预训练（CPT），以解决领域适应中大模型训练数据获取困难和计算成本高的问题，在金融领域和长期记忆保持上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续预训练（CPT）虽广泛用于大语言模型的领域适应，但面临两大挑战：难以获取大规模领域特定数据集，以及高昂的计算成本。

Method: 提出TELLME方法，利用测试增强学习（TEL）原理，在训练过程中引入测验（quizzes）来提升模型训练效率，并将该原理与持续预训练（CPT）相结合，促进高效的领域知识获取和长期记忆保持。

Result: 在金融领域，TELLME相比现有方法最高提升23.6%；在长期记忆保持方面提升9.8%。

Conclusion: TELLME通过将测试增强学习与持续预训练集成，有效缓解了CPT在领域适应中的数据获取困难和计算成本高的问题，同时提升了领域知识获取效率和长期记忆保持能力。

Abstract: Continual pre-training (CPT) has been widely adopted as a method for domain adaptation in large language models. However, CPT has consistently been accompanied by challenges, such as the difficulty of acquiring large-scale domain-specific datasets and high computational costs. In this study, we propose a novel method called Test-Enhanced Learning for Language Model Enrichment (TELLME) to alleviate these issues. TELLME leverages the TestEnhanced Learning (TEL) principle, whereby the model's training efficiency is improved using quizzes during training. It integrates this principle with CPT, thereby promoting efficient domain-specific knowledge acquisition and long-term memory retention. Experimental results demonstrate that TELLME outperforms existing methods by up to 23.6% in the financial domain and achieves a 9.8% improvement in long-term memory retention.

</details>


### [135] [Hybrid Gated Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11805)
*Zekun Zhou,Ruobing Xie,Lanrui Wang,Weixuan Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Hybrid Gated Attention (HyGA)框架，通过三种门控策略融合注意力多阶段的多样信息，从多个视角构建元素级/头级门控，捕获头内和跨头的信息交互，并辅以低秩矩阵分解和可学习注意力汇聚来提升训练效率与稳定性，在多种骨干网络和基准上全面超越Gated attention，实现了更优的效果-效率边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 门控注意力是缓解注意力汇聚（attention sink）问题并增强注意力表示能力的有效方法，但现有方法在效果-效率帕累托前沿上仍有进一步提升空间。作者希望在门控注意力的基础上进一步扩展其适用性和性能边界。

Method: HyGA包含三种门控策略，分别利用注意力多个阶段（如Q/K/V、注意力分数、注意力输出等）的多样化信息，协同构建元素级（element-wise）和头级（head-wise）门控，从多角度捕获头内（intra-head）和跨头（cross-head）信息交互，提供多源调制信号。此外引入低秩矩阵分解以提升训练效率，并加入可学习注意力汇聚以增强训练稳定性。

Result: 在广泛使用的基准数据集上基于不同骨干网络进行实验，HyGA在训练损失和各类下游任务性能上均全面优于Gated attention；在不同计算成本下均取得最佳性能，并通过综合模型分析验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: HyGA作为一种更有效、更高效且更稳定的注意力机制，为注意力门控方向提供了新的设计思路，验证了通过多阶段信息融合和多视角门控可以显著增强注意力表示能力。

Abstract: Gated attention is an effective approach to mitigate attention sinks and enhance the representational capacity of attention. To further extend its effectiveness-efficiency Pareto frontier, we propose a Hybrid Gated Attention (HyGA) framework that contains three types of gating strategies. Specifically, these gates leverage diverse information from multiple stages of attention, and collaboratively build element-wise/head-wise gating from multiple perspectives, capturing intra-head and cross-head information interactions. Through our hybrid gating components, HyGA could provide multi-source modulation signals, enabling more comprehensive control over information flow and improving the representational capacity of attention. We also introduce low-rank matrix decomposition and learnable attention sink to further enhance training efficiency and stability. In experiments, we evaluate HyGA on widely-used benchmarks based on different backbones. The experimental results show that our HyGA comprehensively improves both training loss and various downstream performances compared with Gated attention. HyGA has also been verified to achieve the best performance at different computation costs, with comprehensive model analyses for better understanding. The proposed HyGA sheds light on a more effective, efficient, and stable attention mechanism.

</details>


### [136] [Located but Not Releasable: Silent Gate Inversion and Bounded Linear Release](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11822)
*Xining Xun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对"检测-定位-释放"（detect-localize-release）完整流水线进行全预注册压力测试，结果发现：定位阶段成功，但门控（gating）在分布外完全失效（检测器反转），线性释放（linear release）受到上限约束（剂量-反应曲线远低于预注册裕度）。两种失败可分离，且均不推翻定位的成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有大量研究报道语言模型表征了任务相关的潜在结构却未能使用它。但这些结构一旦被定位，能否真正转化为行为，这一端到端问题鲜有测试。本文旨在对完整的"检测-定位-释放"流水线进行严格的前瞻性（preregistered）压力测试，检验已知的抑制现象（潜在因果结构存在但行为上未使用）能否通过干预真正释放。

Method: 在25.7M规模的transformer上训练因果证据判别任务，采用完全预注册协议（所有阈值、声明模板、决策树分支在数据产生前哈希归档）。完整流水线包含三个阶段：(i)检测器（detector）用于识别需要释放的世界；(ii)定位（localization），在中间层的观察证据通道施加干预以恢复目标行为；(iii)释放（release），即门控注入或无条件线性注入。

Result: 三项发现：(i)定位成功：中间层的观察证据通道干预恢复被抑制世界的行为（配对释放优势0.563和0.854，97.5%置信区间排除零，最佳位点释放率0.889）；(ii)门控分布外失败：标定在零个OOD世界触发的检测器，在6.9-7.3%的ID代际和2400个真正需要释放的代际中触发0次，完全反转，将门控流水线静默降级为基础模型；(iii)线性释放受限：无条件逐实例线性注入产生单调剂量-反应曲线，但平台远低于预注册裕度（截距0.382→0.311→0.264 vs 阈值≤0.08），逐实例自适应仅增加±0.03。

Conclusion: 失败被双重定位：检测器在分布外完全反转，且该位点与分辨率下所有线性释放方向家族距充分性存在界定的上界。两种失败可分离，均不推翻定位的成功。所有数字均可追溯至发布审计链中的哈希化工件，确保可复现性。

Abstract: A growing body of work reports that language models represent task-relevant latent structure that they fail to use. Whether such structure, once located, can be converted into behavior is a separate question that is rarely tested end to end. We submit the complete pipeline -- detect, localize, and release -- to a fully preregistered stress test on a 25.7M transformer trained on causal-evidence discrimination, where a known suppression phenomenon (latent causal structure present but behaviorally unused) has previously been documented. Every threshold, claim template, and decision-tree branch was hashed and archived before any corresponding data existed. Three findings. (i) Localization succeeds: interventions at observation-evidence channels of mid layers restore target behavior on otherwise-suppressed worlds (paired release advantages $0.563$ and $0.854$, 97.5% CIs excluding zero; best-site release rate $0.889$). (ii) Gating fails out of distribution: a detector calibrated to trigger on zero out-of-distribution calibration worlds triggers on 6.9-7.3% of held-out in-distribution generations and on zero of the 2,400 held-out generations that actually need it -- a complete inversion that silently reduces the gated pipeline to its base model. (iii) Linear release is capped: removing the gate and injecting a per-instance linear direction unconditionally yields a monotone dose-response that plateaus far below the preregistered release margin (intercept $0.382 \to 0.311 \to 0.264$ vs. threshold $\le 0.08$); per-instance adaptivity adds less than $\pm 0.03$. The failure is doubly located: the detector is OOD-inverted, and the entire family of linear release directions at this site and resolution is bounded away from sufficiency. The two failures are dissociable, and neither overturns localization. Every number traces to a hashed artifact in the released audit chain.

</details>


### [137] [When the Knowledge Base Becomes the Gold Standard: Measuring Resource-Shared Evaluation Loops in Entity-Level Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11843)
*Jinhyung Bae,Dain Kil,Seongmin Oh,Seungmin Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现低资源历史领域实体翻译评估中存在"自我指涉循环"：用注入系统的同一个知识库（KB）作为评估金标准，导致指标衡量的是指令遵循度而非翻译质量。通过使用韩国国史编纂委员会独立标注的527个专有名词作为对照，发现仅31.1%的标注位于注入管线之外，KB注入的增益仅存在于与金标准共享资源的片段中，独立片段上增益为零或为负。后注入保留能力聚类在狭窄的0.910-0.996区间，且该指标能在模型内复制、模型间区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 《承政院日记》作为联合国教科文组织世界记忆文献，仅完成37.4%的翻译，人名误译会直接损坏历史事实。低资源历史领域缺乏专家金标准用于实体翻译评估，实践者用知识库替代金标准，但该KB与注入系统的资源是同一个，导致评分自我指涉、指标衡量的是指令遵循而非翻译质量。作者旨在量化并揭示这个循环。

Method: 使用韩国国史编纂委员会的专家人名标注作为独立于注入管道的金标准，固定实体集不变，仅改变正确答案的来源（注入资源内/外）。对527个专家标注的人名提及进行分析，并通过四模型差分差分（difference-in-differences）分析KB注入增益，同时在去除构建过滤器的独立样本上检验指标的可复制性和可区分性。

Result: 527个专家标注中仅31.1%在注入管线之外；重叠片段中注入正确率与人译一致率97.8%，独立片段降至70.1%。四模型的差分差分分析显示KB注入收益仅限于与金标准共享注入资源的片段，独立片段上收益为零或负。后注入保留能力集中在0.910-0.996的窄区间，尽管基线能力相差五倍。独立样本上指标在模型内复制（区间重叠）而模型间区分（区间不重叠）。

Conclusion: 当前评估体系存在系统性的自我指涉偏差：以注入KB作为金标准会高估KB注入的实际收益，且最"健康"的评估片段恰恰是循环所支撑的片段。被循环掩盖的独立金标准片段才是真实翻译质量的反映。该指标在模型内可复制、模型间可区分，表明反映的是模型属性而非样本属性，因此改善评估需要建立独立于注入管道的专家金标准。

Abstract: The Seungjeongwon Ilgi, a UNESCO Memory of the World record, is only 37.4% translated, and the most conspicuous failure mode in automatic translation is the person name -- a misread name corrupts the historical fact rather than merely the surface. Low-resource historical domains have no expert gold standard for entity translation, so practitioners substitute a knowledge base (KB) for the gold. That KB is the same resource injected into the system: scoring becomes self-referential and the metric measures instruction compliance rather than translation quality.
  We measure this loop. Using expert person-name annotations from the National Institute of Korean History as a gold independent of the injection pipeline, we hold the entity set fixed and vary only the provenance of the correct reading. Of 527 expert-annotated mentions, only 31.1% lie outside the injection pipeline, and the residual loop is not uniform -- in the overlapping segment the injected reading agrees with the human translation 97.8% of the time against 70.1% in the independent one, so the segment that looks healthiest is the one the loop is holding up.
  Across four models, a difference-in-differences analysis shows the gain from KB injection is confined to the segment whose gold shares the injected resource; in the independent segment it is at or below zero. Post-injection preservation clusters in a narrow 0.910-0.996 band even though baseline capability differs fivefold, so the reported gain is the complement of prior performance and weaker models appear to improve more dramatically. On an independent sample built by removing the construction filter, the measure replicates within model (overlapping intervals) while discriminating between models (non-overlapping intervals) -- it reflects a property of the model, not of the sample.

</details>


### [138] [Total Recall at What Cost? Benchmarking the Serving Cost of Agentic Memory Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11879)
*Natchanon Pollertlam,Witchayut Kornsuwannawit*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对三种记忆系统（Mem0、Hindsight、Mastra观测记忆）与两种参考策略（固定窗口、完整转写重发）在长达400轮对话中的服务成本与答案准确率进行了系统性基准测试（665个LoCoMo问题），发现记忆系统的服务成本无法仅由对话长度和消息大小预测（偏差18-69%），盈亏平衡点因系统而异（从最初几十轮到400轮内永不实现），且没有任何系统在成本与准确率两个维度同时胜出（准确率跨度为21-54%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程对话代理日益依赖记忆系统以避免每轮重发全部对话，但记忆系统在服务成本方面的投入缺乏系统性基准评测，现有认知无法指导实际部署中的成本决策。

Method: 对三种记忆系统（Mem0、Hindsight、Mastra观测记忆）和两种参考策略（固定大小滚动窗口、提交完整转写）进行对比，在两种骨干模型和长达400轮的对话场景中，对665个LoCoMo问题同时测量每次成本与答案准确率，并辅以回归分析和盈亏平衡（break-even）分析。

Result: （1）记忆系统的服务成本无法仅由对话长度和消息大小预测，回归模型与参考策略吻合但对记忆系统偏差达18-69%，成本由内部记忆行为驱动；（2）盈亏平衡分析显示记忆系统是否及何时比完整转写更便宜高度依赖系统与骨干模型，最便宜的在前几十轮即扭转，最贵的在400轮内始终未实现；（3）没有系统在两个维度同时占优，准确率跨度为21-54%，骨干模型的选择对成本的影响与记忆系统本身相当。

Conclusion: 记忆系统的服务成本由其内部记忆行为主导而非仅由对话规模决定，成本-准确的取舍高度依赖特定系统与骨干模型的组合，部署者需依据具体场景权衡，记忆系统的选择并不能独立于骨干模型来决定成本策略。

Abstract: Long-running conversational agents increasingly rely on a memory system to avoid resending the whole conversation each turn, yet how much that costs to serve has received little systematic benchmarking. We compare three memory systems (Mem0, Hindsight, and Mastra Observational Memory) against two reference strategies -- a fixed-size rolling window and resubmitting the full transcript -- across two backbones and conversations of up to 400 turns, pairing every cost measurement with answer accuracy on 665 LoCoMo questions. First, a memory system's serving cost cannot be predicted from conversation length and message size alone: a regression that tracks the two reference strategies closely misses the memory systems by 18-69%, their cost driven instead by internal memory behavior. Second, a break-even analysis shows that whether -- and when -- a memory system becomes cheaper to serve than the full transcript is highly sensitive to the system and the backbone, from the first tens of turns for the cheapest to never within 400 turns for the most expensive. Third, no system wins on both axes: accuracy spans 21-54%, and the backbone choice drives cost as much as the memory system does.

</details>


### [139] [LazyTrain: Limited-resource Allocation toward Zero-waste Yield Optimization in Large Language Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11919)
*Xiaojun Wu,Cehao Yang,Honghao Liu,Xueyuan Lin,Xuhui Jiang,Chengjin Xu,Jia Li,Jian Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LazyTrain是一个针对层流式执行器的优化层，将检查点选择、激活放置、重计算和CPU-GPU-NVMe通信重叠建模为混合整数调度问题，并结合8位优化器状态与快速梯度裁剪，在有限硬件上提升大语言模型训练效率。在H800和RTX 3090上分别获得约1.24×的TFLOPS提升和更大批量规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在有限硬件上训练本质上是GPU计算、主机内存、PCIe传输和存储带宽之间的调度问题。现有系统（如MegaTrain）虽然通过CPU主控的层流式执行器实现单GPU训练大模型，但固定的检查点策略和放置启发式仍使通信暴露在关键路径上，导致训练效率受损。

Method: LazyTrain将检查点选择、激活放置、重计算以及CPU-GPU-NVMe通信重叠形式化为一个混合整数调度问题（Mixed-Integer Scheduling Problem），在训练前求解该策略并在训练中执行。此外，提出混合8位算子（Hybrid 8-bit operator）：将8位优化器状态与快速梯度裁剪耦合，状态压缩降低优化器显存占用，快速裁剪抵消CPU侧更新的额外开销。

Result: 在H800上，从Qwen2.5-3B到Qwen3.6-27B，LazyTrain相比匹配的基线运行提升约1.24×的持续TFLOPS；RTX 3090上每种模型规模的最大可行批量大小增加1。在Qwen3.6-27B H800 MetaMathQA主实验上达到219.95 TFLOPS、1361 tokens/s（batch size 72），GPU显存峰值68.84GB，完整评估集上获得95.42%的精确匹配准确率。

Conclusion: LazyTrain通过全局调度优化和混合8位算子，有效缓解了有限硬件上大语言模型训练中通信暴露于关键路径的问题，在吞吐量和批量规模上均取得显著提升，代码已开源。

Abstract: Training large language models on limited hardware is increasingly a scheduling problem across GPU compute, host memory, PCIe transfer, and storage bandwidth. Existing offloading systems reduce GPU residency, and MegaTrain shows that a CPU-master layer-streaming executor can train large models on a single GPU, but fixed checkpointing and placement heuristics still leave communication exposed on the critical path. We propose LazyTrain, an optimization layer over a layer-streaming executor. LazyTrain formulates checkpoint selection, activation placement, recomputation, and CPU-GPU-NVMe communication overlap as a mixed-integer scheduling problem, then executes the solved policy during training. It further couples 8-bit optimizer states with fast gradient clipping as a single Hybrid 8-bit operator: state compression reduces optimizer-state memory, while fast clipping counteracts the additional CPU-side update overhead. Across H800 experiments from Qwen2.5-3B to Qwen3.6-27B, LazyTrain improves sustained TFLOPS over matched baselines runs by approximately 1.24$\times$; RTX 3090 experiments likewise increase the maximum feasible batch size by one at each model scale. In the primary Qwen3.6-27B H800 MetaMathQA run, LazyTrain reaches 219.95 TFLOPS and 1361 tokens/s at batch size 72, peaks at 68.84\,GB of GPU memory, and obtains 95.42\% exact-match accuracy on the full evaluation split. The source code is available at https://github.com/DataArcTech/LazyTrain.

</details>


### [140] [LODESTAR: Trustworthy Entropy Is Navigated, Not Merely Measured -- Reinforced Polarizer Keeps a Frozen LLM from Being Confidently Misled by the Wrong Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11922)
*Po-Jen Ko,Che-Cheng Wu,Hung-Chun Hsu,Li-Yang Chang,Chuan-Ju Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LODESTAR通过强化学习训练一个固定的自然语言"极化器"字符串插入到冻结的respondent LLM提示中，以降低检索增强问答中误导性段落的负面影响。在5,008个问题上取得最高平均F1（0.5339）、精确匹配（0.4136）和GPT-4o评判分数（0.6435），在70个方法×数据集单元上全部胜出14种已发布配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于熵的选择规则（如最低答案token熵）在检索增强问答中虽有提升，但在遇到误导性段落时会让respondent LLM自信地犯错——熵越低反而越危险。需要一种能通过干预上下文（而非权重）来改善这种情况的方法。

Method: LODESTAR采用强化学习离线训练一个"极化器"（polarizer）——一段固定的自然语言字符串，插入到respondent的提示中而非权重中。训练标签从gold answers和两个LLM法官离线构建，推理时不读取任何gold answers或法官。核心思想是通过评分文本干预（插入极化器）在第三方冻结respondent中诱导的不确定性来比较同一问题下的候选答案。

Result: 在5,008个问题上，LODESTAR达到最高平均F1 0.5339（对比基线0.5148）、最高精确匹配0.4136、最高GPT-4o法官分数0.6435。三种子均值的F1在70个方法×数据集单元中全部胜出14种已发布配置，且均具配对显著性。域内和域外均有提升；消融实验显示极化器使误导性段落的读取率从30.3%降至26.0%。

Conclusion: 通过干预上下文而非模型权重，可以有效缓解熵基选择规则的失败模式——让respondent在误导性段落上不再"自信地犯错"。LODESTAR是首个通过第三方冻结respondent诱导的不确定性来评分文本干预的方法，在无需gold answers的推理条件下显著超越所有现有选择器。

Abstract: Predictive-distribution entropy makes a strong selection rule in retrieval-augmented question answering: across five QA benchmarks, keeping the candidate answer that a frozen respondent LLM produces with the lowest answer-token entropy lifts mean answer $F_1$ from 0.4769 to 0.5148 over the retriever's top-ranked passage, with no gold answers. Yet this lowest-entropy rule, which prior entropy-based selectors adopt, fails in a specific and consequential way: a misleading passage makes the respondent confidently wrong, driving its entropy down precisely where the signal looks most trustworthy. We show that the failure comes from the passage the respondent reads -- and the context that passage is read in is an input we can intervene on. We introduce LODESTAR, to our knowledge the first method to score a text intervention by the uncertainty it induces in a third-party frozen respondent, compared across one question's candidates. LODESTAR uses reinforcement learning to train, once and offline, a polarizer -- a short fixed natural-language string inserted into the respondent's prompt and never into its weights; its training labels are built offline from gold answers and two LLM judges, and inference reads neither. Evaluating every competing selector under the same frozen respondent and the same candidate pools on 5,008 questions, LODESTAR attains the highest mean $F_1$ of any inference-ready selector (0.5148 to 0.5339), the highest exact match (0.4136), and the highest GPT-4o judge score of the frozen-respondent configurations judged (0.6435); its three-seed mean wins all 70 method-by-dataset $F_1$ cells against fourteen published configurations while remaining paired-significant against every one. The gain holds both in-domain and out-of-domain, and ablating the polarizer shows it is what makes the respondent read a misleading passage less often (26.0% against 30.3%).

</details>


### [141] [Spark-to-Paper: End-to-End Research Paper Generation as a Composable Skill](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11924)
*Zhuoyang Qian,Biao Wu,Yiran Wang,Chris D Yan,Desan Dai,Liangwei Zheng,Jin Jiang,Junsheng Zhang,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Spark-to-Paper，一个在现有编码助手中以十三个可组合技能实现的端到端研究论文生成系统。该系统将模型判断与确定性操作分离，将实验规划与报告分离，并引入自我批判和完整性检查来约束"自我反驳循环"失败模式。系统在八个受控研究主题上达到99.5%引用有效性和96.4%图表可编辑性，将捏造检测率从14%提升至92%，平均每篇论文消耗1190万token、花费8.1美元、耗时3.2小时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将研究想法转化为完整论文需要远超文本生成的能力：系统必须检索文献、设计和执行实验、根据证据修正主张、生成可发表的图表，并在长生成过程中保持一致性。现有系统缺乏这种端到端能力，且多依赖独立的智能体平台或编排服务。

Method: Spark-to-Paper在现有编码助手内部实现为十三个可组合技能，无需独立智能体平台。核心设计包括：(1)将基于模型的判断与可直接执行和检查的确定性操作分离；(2)将实验规划与报告分离，确保在观测结果前指定所需证据，并根据测量结果修正手稿主张；(3)结合确定性完整性检查与自我批判，约束自我反驳循环（重复实验持续否定原始研究目标的失败模式）；(4)通过程序化绘图生成实验结果的矢量图，通过基于代码的重建生成方法示意图。

Result: 在八个受控研究主题上，系统达到99.5%引用有效性和96.4%图表可编辑性。受控消融实验将捏造检测率从单次生成的14%提升到完整完整性栈的92%，对抗性审查达到74%精确率。完整系统平均使用1190万token，每篇手稿成本8.1美元，平均耗时3.2小时。

Conclusion: 研究表明，端到端研究论文生成可以以轻量、可组合的工作流方式在现有编码助手中实现，同时保持实验证据在主张接受、修正或放弃过程中的核心地位。

Abstract: Turning a research idea into a complete paper requires more than text generation: the system must retrieve literature, design and execute experiments, revise claims according to evidence, produce publication-ready figures, and maintain consistency across a long generation process. We present Spark-to-Paper, an end-to-end research paper generation system implemented as thirteen composable skills inside an existing coding assistant, without requiring a separate agent platform or orchestration service. Spark-to-Paper separates model-based judgment from deterministic operations that can be directly executed and checked. It further separates experiment planning from reporting, so that required evidence is specified before results are observed and manuscript claims are revised according to measured outcomes. To improve reliability over long research trajectories, the system combines deterministic integrity checks with self-critique and bounds a failure mode we call the Self-Refutation Loop, in which repeated experiments continue to reject the original research objective. Spark-to-Paper also produces editable vector figures through programmatic plotting for experimental results and code-based reconstruction for generated method diagrams. Across eight controlled research topics, Spark-to-Paper achieves 99.5% citation validity and 96.4% figure editability. A controlled ablation increases fabrication detection from 14% for a single-pass draft to 92% with the full integrity and review stack, while adversarial review achieves 74% precision. The full system uses 11.9M tokens, costs $8.1 per manuscript, and requires 3.2 hours on average. These results show that end-to-end research paper generation can be implemented as a lightweight, composable workflow inside existing coding assistants while keeping experimental evidence central to how claims are accepted, revised, or abandoned.

</details>


### [142] [Accuracy and Order Sensitivity Diverge Under Label-Free Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11947)
*Karl Hanna,Chen Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大规模语言模型在多选题中的选项顺序敏感性偏差。作者测试了两种防止模型看到选项标签的答题策略（生成-匹配法和独立评分法），发现它们都不能可靠地提高准确性。通过分解实验揭示瓶颈在于隐藏选项本身，而非匹配步骤。仅当展示所有选项并配合LLM匹配器时才与基线持平，但完全消除位置影响仍不能保证准确率提升，而循环置换反而常能改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多选题基准被广泛用于评估大语言模型，但多选题得分将模型的知识与选项顺序敏感性混为一谈，导致其作为模型知识度量指标不可靠。作者希望探索一种方法，通过防止模型在作答前看到选项标签来消除位置偏差，进而提升模型在多选题上的真实表现。

Method: 作者设计了两种去偏策略：(1) 生成-然后-匹配法（generation-then-matching），模型先不看到选项生成答案，再用匹配器对齐；(2) 独立选项评分法（scoring options in isolation），每个选项单独评分，从构造上消除位置偏差。此外进行了完整的分解实验来定位性能瓶颈，并测试了两阶段提示中的两种去偏度量方法：召回不平衡的聚合度量与每问题级别的顺序敏感性直接度量。

Result: 两种去偏策略均未能可靠地提升准确率。分解实验表明瓶颈在于隐藏选项这一操作本身，而非匹配步骤。唯一稳定达到基线的配置是向模型展示所有选项并搭配LLM匹配器。完全消除位置影响并不能可靠带来准确率增益，而循环置换选项顺序反而经常能改善准确率。两种顺序敏感性度量方法均未能展示可靠的去偏效果。

Conclusion: 研究发现，试图通过隐藏选项标签来消除多选题中的位置偏差并不能可靠地提升模型性能，且现有去偏度量指标不足以指示有效的偏差消除。这提示多选题评估的可靠性问题比预想更复杂，选项顺序偏差与模型知识表现之间的关系需要更深入的研究。

Abstract: Multiple-choice benchmarks are widely used to evaluate large language models, but MCQ scores conflate knowledge with sensitivity to option order, which makes them unreliable measures of model knowledge. In this paper, we test whether preventing a model from seeing option labels while committing to an answer removes positional influence and, in turn, improves performance. We evaluate two different strategies for mitigating bias. The first uses a generation-then-matching approach, and the second scores options in isolation, which is positionally unbiased by construction. Neither reliably improves accuracy. A complete decomposition shows that the bottleneck is withholding options, not the matching step. The only configuration that consistently matches the baseline is the one that shows the model all options paired with an LLM matcher. However, eliminating positional influence entirely still does not reliably yield accuracy gains, while cyclic permutation often improves them. For two-stage prompting, an aggregate measure of recall imbalance and a direct per-question measure of order sensitivity both fail to show reliable debiasing.

</details>


### [143] [Benchmarking Trustworthiness of SLMs: Pre-trained vs. Compressed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11981)
*Haokun Lin,Kaijie Zhu,Haobo Xu,Yichen Wu,Zhichao Lu,Qingfu Zhang,Zhenan Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对小型语言模型（SLM）的可信度进行了多维度综合评估，发现量化比剪枝更能保持可信度；压缩可靠的大模型得到的SLM在可信度和适应性上优于从零训练的小模型；知识蒸馏可进一步提升SLM的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SLM作为LLM的高效替代方案在资源受限场景中具有潜力，但随着模型在真实应用中的广泛集成，确保其可信度（公平性、鲁棒性、隐私、伦理）成为关键问题。然而如何构建可信SLM仍未被充分探索，现有研究缺乏对SLM可信度的系统性评估。

Method: 构建了涵盖公平性、鲁棒性、隐私和伦理多个维度的综合评估框架；系统比较了两种SLM构建路径（从零训练小型模型 vs 压缩预训练模型）下的可信度表现；分别考察了剪枝和量化两种压缩方法对可信度的影响；并研究了从可信教师模型进行知识蒸馏对SLM可靠性的提升效果。

Result: 实验发现：量化相较于剪枝能更有效地保持模型可信度；通过量化压缩可靠的大模型可以产生比从零训练的小模型具有更优可信度和适应性；知识蒸馏从可信教师模型可进一步增强SLM的可靠性。

Conclusion: 研究为可信SLM的开发与部署提供了实践指导，表明通过压缩可靠大模型（特别是量化）并辅以知识蒸馏是一种构建可信SLM的有效路径，为未来相关研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Small Language Models (SLMs) have emerged as a more efficient alternative to traditional Large Language Models (LLMs), offering promising potential in resource-constrained scenarios. Existing approaches to building SLMs typically follow two paths: training compact models from scratch, or compressing larger pre-trained models using methods such as pruning, quantization, or distillation. As language models become increasingly integrated into real-world applications, ensuring their trustworthiness has become a critical concern. However, how to build trustworthy SLMs remains an underexplored question. In this work, we present a comprehensive evaluation of SLM trustworthiness across multiple dimensions, including fairness, robustness, privacy, and ethics. We first examine the effects of pruning and quantization, and find that quantization is significantly more effective in preserving trustworthiness compared to pruning. More importantly, we demonstrate that compressing a reliable large model via quantization can produce SLMs with superior trustworthiness and adaptability compared to using small models trained from scratch. Furthermore, knowledge distillation from trustworthy teacher models can further enhance the reliability of SLMs. We hope our findings provide practical guidance and a foundation for future research into the development and deployment of trustworthy small language models.

</details>


### [144] [Asymptotic Risk Calibration for Selective Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12008)
*Shufan Lin,Sijin Dong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出A-CRC-QA，一个面向不确定性感知的选择性问答的后验校准框架，通过将选择条件错误控制重构为线性期望约束，并应用单调化的经验风险校准过程（受置信风险控制启发），实现对接受答案错误率的统计控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型可能生成流畅但不正确的答案，因此不确定性量化对可靠问答至关重要。然而，启发式不确定性评分无法完美区分正确与错误预测，且直接应用固定不确定性阈值无法对接受答案的错误率提供统计控制。

Method: A-CRC-QA将选择条件错误控制重构为线性期望约束，并应用单调化的经验风险校准过程（受置信风险控制启发）。由于实例级损失关于接受阈值一般非单调，该框架以渐近风险控制为目标而非有限样本风险控制。该方法是模型无关的，无需额外训练，可与不同不确定性估计器结合。

Result: 在CoQA和MedMCQA上的实验表明，该方法适用于开放式和封闭式问答任务，与未校准和基于置信度边界的基线相比，在接受答案可靠性与答案保留率之间取得了更优的权衡。

Conclusion: A-CRC-QA提供了一种无需额外训练、模型无关的后验校准方案，能够对选择性问答中接受答案的错误率进行统计控制，在可靠性与覆盖率之间实现更优权衡。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) may generate fluent but incorrect answers, making uncertainty quantification important for reliable question answering. However, heuristic uncertainty scores cannot perfectly distinguish correct predictions from incorrect ones, and directly applying a fixed uncertainty threshold provides no statistical control over the error rate among accepted answers. To address this limitation, we propose A-CRC-QA, a post-hoc calibration framework for uncertainty-aware selective question answering. The proposed method reformulates selection-conditioned error control as a linear expectation constraint and applies a monotonized empirical-risk calibration procedure inspired by conformal risk control. Since the resulting instance-wise loss is generally non-monotone with respect to the acceptance threshold, our framework targets asymptotic rather than finite-sample risk control. A-CRC-QA is model-agnostic, requires no additional training, and can be combined with different uncertainty estimators. Experiments on CoQA and MedMCQA demonstrate its applicability to both open-ended and closed-ended question answering, achieving a favorable trade-off between accepted-answer reliability and answer retention compared with uncalibrated and confidence-bound-based baselines.

</details>


### [145] [Poly-Dialectal Neural Machine Translation System for Bangla Regional Dialects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12018)
*Rakib Ullah,Ruhul Islam Rahul,Tanbir Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的孟加拉语多方言神经机器翻译系统，支持12种方言之间的多向翻译（无需标准语中转），构建了迄今最大的孟加拉语多方言平行语料库（51,531个句子对），并基于DoRA微调的BanglaT5模型取得了最先进的翻译性能（29.26 BLEU，57.26 chrF++），通过与NLLB-200和mBART-50的对比验证了其优越性，最终以INT8量化模型形式部署为开源Web应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉语拥有超过2.4亿使用者，存在显著的地区方言差异（在音系、形态和词汇层面与标准口语孟加拉语SCB差异巨大），但现有NMT架构和LLM普遍假设语言分布同质化，导致低资源地区方言翻译性能严重退化。此外，现有系统通常需要经由标准语作为枢轴进行间接翻译，缺乏直接的多方言翻译能力。

Method: 该系统首先编译了包含51,531个非空平行句对的孟加拉语多方言平行语料库，覆盖12种方言，其中包括5个此前未被研究的方言的2,500条经专家验证的双向平行句对。随后，在Weight-Decomposed Low-Rank Adaptation（DoRA）参数高效微调框架下，对BanglaT5序列到序列架构进行微调；同时进行了跨方言迁移分析和数据集规模扩展研究，以建立低资源方言适应的经验阈值；最终将模型INT8量化后部署为开源Web应用。

Result: 微调后的BanglaT5模型在12大方言翻译任务上取得了29.26 BLEU和57.26 chrF++的最先进性能，优于NLLB-200（615M参数）和mBART-50（611M参数）等更强基座模型，同时保持了形态一致性。系统还提供了跨方言迁移分析和数据规模阈值的研究结论。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了参数高效微调（DoRA）结合多方言平行数据的策略能够在低资源方言翻译场景中超越大规模预训练多语言模型，为孟加拉语方言数字化和边缘方言社区的语言包容性提供了实际部署方案，并公开了数据集和模型以促进该领域的进一步研究。

Abstract: Regional dialectal variation poses a fundamental challenge to natural language processing (NLP) in Bangla, where over 240 million speakers communicate across diverse regional variants that diverge significantly from Standard Colloquial Bangla (SCB) in phonology, morphology, and lexicon. Contemporary neural machine trans- lation (NMT) architectures and large language models (LLMs) predominantly as- sume a homogeneous language distribution, resulting in severe performance degra- dation when translating low-resource regional dialects. In this work, we present a unified Poly-Dialectal Neural Machine Translation System capable of multi-directional translation across 12 Bangla regional dialects without routing through an inter- mediary standard pivot. We compile the largest multi-dialect parallel corpus for Bangla to date, comprising 51,531 non-null parallel sentence pairs across 12 di- alects, incorporating 2,500 expert-verified, bidirectional parallel sentence pairs for five previously unaddressed dialects. Evaluating sequence-to-sequence architec- tures under Weight-Decomposed Low-Rank Adaptation (DoRA), our fine-tuned BanglaT5 model achieves state-of-the-art translation performance (29.26 BLEU, 57.26 chrF++), outperforming NLLB-200 (615M) and mBART-50 (611M) while preserving morphological coherence. Furthermore, we conduct a systematic cross- dialectal transfer analysis and dataset scaling study, establishing empirical thresh- olds for low-resource dialect adaptation. Finally, we deploy the optimized INT8- quantized model as an open-access web application to promote digital inclusion for marginalized dialect communities. The complete dataset is publicly available at Mendeley Data (https://data.mendeley.com/datasets/v9cf66fk2t/2).

</details>


### [146] [Preference Tree Optimization: Enhancing Goal-Oriented Dialogue with Look-Ahead Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12062)
*Lior Baruch,Moshe Butman,Kfir Bar,Doron Friedman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出偏好树优化（PTO）框架，通过偏好树与前瞻模拟生成偏好数据，结合直接偏好优化（DPO）迭代改进面向目标的对话代理模型，在动机性访谈（MI）领域显著提升对话性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发支持多轮、目标导向对话的对话系统仍面临重大挑战，尤其是在数据稀缺的专业领域。现有的对话代理难以在没有充足标注数据的情况下做出良好的决策，亟需一种能够在低资源条件下迭代提升代理模型能力的框架。

Method: 提出偏好树优化（PTO）框架，核心包括：1）偏好树与前瞻（Preference Tree with Look-Ahead）方法生成偏好数据，利用虚拟病人和神谕评估器模拟对话；2）结合直接偏好优化（DPO）算法，在迭代训练循环中增强代理的决策能力；3）通过不同深度的前瞻配置探索对话策略空间。

Result: 实验表明，PTO框架显著提升了代理在MI领域目标导向对话中的性能。经PTO训练的模型在会话满意度和工作联盟等关键指标上持续优于基线；融入前瞻模拟的模型展现出更好的长期规划和更有效的对话策略，更深的前瞻配置产生最稳定且得分最高的结果。

Conclusion: PTO框架有效解决了专业领域数据稀缺的问题，通过迭代式偏好数据生成与优化，为面向目标对话代理的发展提供了可行且高效的新途径，尤其在动机性访谈等特定领域具有明显优势。

Abstract: Developing dialogue systems capable of engaging in multi-turn, goal-oriented conversations remains a significant challenge, especially in specialized domains with limited data. This research proposes a novel framework called Preference Tree Optimization (PTO), designed to iteratively improve agent models in such dialogue systems, by generating preference data using a method called Preference Tree with Look-Ahead. Focusing on Motivational Interviewing (MI) -- a counseling technique aimed at facilitating behavioral change -- we leverage virtual patients and an oracle evaluator to simulate conversations and generate rich preference datasets. By combining this method with Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), we aim to enhance the agent's decision-making capabilities over iterative training cycles. The proposed framework addresses data scarcity and advances the development of more nuanced and effective dialogue systems in goal-oriented domains. Experimental evaluations demonstrate that the PTO framework enhances dialogue agents' performance in goal-oriented conversations within the domain of Motivational Interviewing (MI). Models trained with PTO consistently outperformed the baseline in key metrics such as session satisfaction and working alliance. Additionally, incorporating look-ahead simulations led to improved long-term planning and more effective conversational strategies, with deeper look-ahead configurations yielding the most stable and high-scoring results.

</details>


### [147] [Structuring the Space of Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12113)
*Agnese Daffara,Sebastian Padó,Tanise Ceron*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文综述了自然语言处理中与"视角"（perspective）相关的各种概念（如立场、情感、框架、论证等），定义了区分这些概念的属性，并提出一个沿单一轴线性组织的概念层级体系，帮助研究者选择合适的操作化定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一事件可以从不同视角被报道，NLP领域从文本分析到算法优化都在处理视角问题。虽然已有多种操作性概念（立场、情感、框架、论证等）被用来捕捉文本中的视角，但这些概念之间的精确关系尚不清晰，缺乏深层理论理解会阻碍视角相关研究的有效性。

Method: 作者通过系统综述NLP中的视角研究空间，定义了一组用于区分视角相关概念的属性，基于这些属性提出一个概念层级结构，将各视角概念沿单一轴线线性组织。

Result: 提出了一套区分视角概念属性及一个线性概念层级体系，并展示了该层级如何帮助研究者导航该领域，选择与其具体研究目标一致的操作化定义。

Conclusion: 这种基于原则的概念层级结构能够为NLP研究者提供理论指导，帮助他们更有针对性地选择和使用视角相关概念，从而推动视角研究的发展。

Abstract: The same event can be reported from different perspectives depending on the experiences, background, and beliefs of the writer or speaker. A variety of NLP areas engage with perspectives, spanning from text analysis to algorithm optimization. A wide range of operative concepts (such as stances, sentiment, frames, and arguments) has been used to capture perspectives in texts, however the precise relationships among those concepts remain unclear. Arguably, a deeper theoretical understanding of these concepts would empower more effective research on perspectives. In this paper, we address this gap by reviewing the space of perspectives in NLP and defining a set of properties that help distinguishing perspective-related concepts. Our analysis leads us to posit a hierarchy which organizes these concepts linearly along a single axis. Finally, we show how this principled conceptual hierarchy can help researchers navigate the field and select operationalizations of perspective that align with their specific research objectives.

</details>


### [148] [QV-PIC: Query-Aware Visual Position-Independent Caching for Efficient RAG Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12121)
*Yilin Liu,Rui Meng,Wangze Ni,Jianxin Yan,Heng Cao,Libin Zheng,Peng Cheng,Jinfei Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出QV-PIC，一种基于模型原生模板的查询感知双分辨率视觉缓存复用框架，通过离线编译视觉缓存消除在线重计算，并在线上用低分辨率保留全局上下文、用高分辨率按查询相关性分数恢复细粒度文本证据，从而在保持视觉压缩效率的同时弥合与文本PIC的质量差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG中重复的前缀预填充导致冗余计算；现有PIC方案虽能复用KV缓存，但文本token体积大、效率受限。将文本渲染为图像可压缩视觉token，但渲染图像PIC因缓存间上下文不匹配和视觉压缩丢失细粒度文本证据而质量下降严重。已有修复方法仅通过选择性重计算处理前者，存在在线计算开销且无法恢复损失的文字细节。

Method: QV-PIC框架：离线阶段在模型原生chat-template前缀下编译视觉缓存，提升PIC质量且无需在线重计算；在线阶段采用双分辨率策略——低分辨率保留全局上下文，高分辨率通过累积查询相关性分数在预算内恢复细粒度文本证据，兼顾视觉压缩的效率优势。

Result: 在六个任务上，QV-PIC相对原始渲染图像PIC平均F1提升21.6点，弥合了与文本PIC的差距，并优于优化后的文本PIC 2.58 F1，同时TTFT降低17.2%；相对于全量预填充，TTFT降低83.8%。

Conclusion: 通过查询感知的双分辨率视觉缓存复用与模型原生模板的离线编译策略，QV-PIC在不牺牲效率的前提下，有效解决了渲染图像PIC的质量缺陷，实现了比文本PIC更优的质量与更低的时延。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) repeatedly prefills identical text chunks across queries, incurring redundant computations. Position-Independent Caching (PIC) mitigates it by reusing precomputed Key-Value (KV) across positions, but its efficiency is constrained by the large volume of text tokens. Rendering text chunks as images can compress the text into fewer visual tokens, but the rendered-image PIC suffers more severe quality degradation than the text PIC. This representation-specific gap primarily arises from contextual mismatches across independently compiled caches and the loss of fine-grained textual evidence during visual compression. Existing PIC repair methods mainly address the former through selective recomputation, but they incur online computation and cannot recover lost textual details. We propose QV-PIC, a query-aware dual-resolution PIC reuse framework guided by model-native templates. Offline, QV-PIC compiles visual caches under the model's native chat-template prefix, improving PIC quality without online recomputation. Online, it preserves global context with low resolution and restores fine-grained textual evidence within a high-resolution budget by cumulative query relevance scores, retaining the efficiency benefit of visual compression. Across six tasks, QV-PIC improves average F1 by 21.6 points over vanilla rendered-image PIC, closes the gap to vanilla text PIC, and surpasses optimized text PIC by 2.58 F1 while reducing TTFT by 17.2\%. Relative to full prefill, it cuts TTFT by 83.8%.

</details>


### [149] [SAG: SQL-Retrieval Augmented Generation with Query-Time Dynamic Hyperedges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12129)
*Yuchao Wu,Junqin Li,XingCheng Liang,Yongjie Chen,Yinghao Liang,Linyuan Mo,Guanxian Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SAG（SQL检索增强生成）提出了一种结构化检索架构，将文档组织为事件-实体索引（而非全局知识图谱），每个块被表示为语义完整的事件与其实体配对，形成保持n元关系的潜在超边。查询时以共享实体作为连接键动态连接相关块，生成查询范围的邻域，同时保留原始块作为证据。在HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA和MuSiQue上均取得最优检索与端到端QA性能，且在推理链越复杂的场景中优势越明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流的密集检索方法在处理结构化约束和多跳推理方面存在固有局限；虽然基于图的方法通过离线构建知识图谱来应对，但会碎片化语义、维护成本高且难以增量更新。因此需要一种既能处理结构化多跳推理、又能避免构建全局知识图谱缺点的检索架构。

Method: SAG将文档组织为事件-实体索引，每个块表示为一个语义完整的事件与其关联实体，构成潜在超边以保留n元关系（不分解为三元组）。查询时，SAG将共享实体视为连接键来关联相关块，动态生成查询范围的邻域，所有证据始终保留原始块的完整性。

Result: 在HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA和MuSiQue三个基准上，SAG在检索和端到端QA性能上均达到最优。在MuSiQue（多跳证据链最难的基准）上达到80.36%的Recall@5，比最强基线高出11.52个百分点。优势随推理链复杂度增加而扩大。

Conclusion: SAG为持续增长的组织化知识提供了高效的检索与推理基础设施，使LLM代理能够在无需维护全局知识图谱的情况下进行多跳结构化推理，同时保持证据的原始语义完整性。

Abstract: While retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has proven effective at giving LLMs access to external knowledge, mainstream dense-retrieval implementations remain inherently limited in handling structured constraints and multi-hop reasoning. Graph-based methods address this by constructing knowledge graphs offline, but they often fragment semantics, incur high maintenance, and complicate incremental updates. We propose SAG (SQL-Retrieval Augmented Generation), a structured retrieval architecture that organizes documents into an event-entity index without building a global knowledge graph. SAG represents each chunk as a semantically complete event paired with its entities, forming a latent hyperedge that preserves n-ary relations without decomposing them into triples. At query time, SAG treats shared entities as join keys to connect related chunks. This dynamically yields a query-scoped neighborhood of events, and yet every piece of evidence remains the original chunk throughout. Experiments on HotpotQA, 2WikiMultiHopQA, and MuSiQue show that SAG achieves the best retrieval and end-to-end QA performance on every benchmark, with gains that widen as reasoning-chain complexity increases. On MuSiQue, where multi-hop evidence chaining is most demanding, SAG reaches 80.36% Recall@5, outperforming the strongest baseline by 11.52 points. This work paves the way for knowledge infrastructure that enables LLM agents to retrieve and reason over continually growing organizational knowledge.

</details>


### [150] [A corpus-specific clinical RAG system matches or outperforms newer frontier LLMs on HealthBench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12138)
*Praveen Reddy,Charuta Mandke,Suvrankar Datta,Sarah Khan,Siddharth Reddy Anthireddy,Shitij Arora,Vishal Singh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: VITA是一个为印度及中低收入国家（LMIC）环境专门构建的检索增强生成（RAG）临床系统。在HealthBench基准上，VITA以51.9%的评分点得分击败了包括GPT-5.4、o4-mini、Gemini 3.1 Pro和Claude Sonnet 4.6等前沿通用大模型；但在使用更现代模型和中性评判者重测后，VITA与GPT-5.5差距缩小至统计上无差异的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对大语言模型与专科临床AI工具的比较研究通常基于高收入国家开发的基准，且只涉及少数系统。作者旨在评估一个专为LMIC环境设计的RAG系统在与前沿通用LLM竞争中的真实表现，并检验其在不同模型代际和评判者下的稳健性。

Method: 使用VITA这一专为印度及LMIC环境构建的RAG系统，从疾病特定指南、印度抗菌药物耐药数据、国家处方集约束和资源有限护理协议组成的策展语料库中检索。在4,023个HealthBench英语问题上以GPT-4.1评判打分；另取500题子集，用当前最新模型（GPT-5.5、Claude Opus 4.8、Gemini 3.5 Pro、Grok 4.3）重测，并由与所有被测系统无血缘关系的开源权重模型DeepSeek-V4-Pro作为中性评判者评分。

Result: 在GPT-4.1评判下，VITA以51.9%的评分点得分排名第一，领先GPT-5.4（46.1%）、o4-mini（44.3%）、Gemini 3.1 Pro（42.6%）和Claude Sonnet 4.6（37.3%），并在45.4%的问题上得分最高。在500题子集重测中，VITA与GPT-5.5在每题平均分上统计无显著差异，但在加权得分和胜出问题数上仍领先；VITA的准确性和完整性优势在神经评判下保持，但沟通得分较低。

Conclusion: 目的性构建的临床RAG系统在开放基准上仍可与前沿通用LLM竞争，语料库特异性作为设计变量能提升回答的接地性（grounding），但以一定的沟通润色为代价。这也说明仅用单一基准和评判者评估会高估RAG系统相对通用模型优势的稳定性。

Abstract: General-purpose large language models (LLMs) have recently been reported to match or exceed specialized clinical AI tools on medical benchmarks, but such comparisons draw on a narrow set of systems and on benchmarks developed largely in high-income settings. We evaluate VITA, a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) system purpose-built for contextual knowledge retrieval in India and other low- and middle-income (LMIC) settings. VITA retrieves from a curated corpus of disease-specific guidelines, India-specific antimicrobial resistance data, national formulary constraints, and resource-limited care protocols; its architecture and corpus are proprietary, but the benchmark, the physician-written rubrics, and our full response and scoring outputs are public for independent verification. On 4,023 English-language HealthBench questions (80.5% of the benchmark), scored with a GPT-4.1 judge, VITA ranked first with 51.9% of possible rubric points, ahead of GPT-5.4 (46.1%), o4-mini (44.3%), Gemini 3.1 Pro (42.6%), and Claude Sonnet 4.6 (37.3%), and scored highest on 45.4% of questions. To test robustness to newer models and judge lineage, a 500-question subset was re-run against current-generation models (GPT-5.5, Claude Opus 4.8, Gemini 3.5 Pro, Grok 4.3) and graded by a neutral open-weight judge (DeepSeek-V4-Pro) sharing no lineage with any system tested. Here the gap narrowed to parity: VITA and GPT-5.5 were statistically indistinguishable on mean per-question score, while VITA led on points-weighted score and won the most questions. VITA's advantages in accuracy and completeness persisted under the neutral judge; its communication scores were lower. These results indicate that a purpose-built clinical RAG system remains competitive with frontier LLMs on an open benchmark, consistent with corpus specificity as a design variable that improves grounding at some cost to communication polish.

</details>


### [151] [Massive Activations in Hybrid Linear Attention Large Language Models: Pre-Attention Spikes and Inter-Spike Plateaus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12149)
*Zunhai Su,Bohan Sun,Xialie Zhuang,Shuibai Zhang,He Xiao,Jing Xiong,Hengyuan Zhang,Zhongzhu Zhou,Tiantian Zhang,Ngai Wong,Chuan-Wei Kuo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了层交错混合线性注意力(linear attention)大语言模型(LLM)中的Massive activations (MAs)现象,发现两种与架构对齐的MA形态:全注意力层之前的"pre-attention spikes"(PAS)和线性注意力层间的"inter-spike plateaus"(ISP)。该组织在五种线性注意力架构、六种混合配置、五个数据域以及1.2B到397B参数规模的开源混合模型中均一致复现。通过受控预训练实验和机制性离群值分析,论文提出统一的生命周期解释——MA取消的时序决定了PAS的局部写-汇-消过程与ISP的延迟取消,并在全注意力极限下恢复全注意力LLM的稳定MA形态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已有研究观察到全注意力LLM中存在Massive activations,但在混合线性注意力(HLA)架构中,由于全注意力与线性注意力层交错堆叠,MA的形态与演化规律尚未被系统研究。理解MA在混合架构中的产生机制对模型可解释性、剪枝策略和架构设计优化至关重要。

Method: 作者系统性地对五种线性注意力架构、六种混合配置、五个数据域及多种参数规模的HLA模型进行MA形态分析;通过受控预训练(1.3B参数的GDN混合模型)研究MA的涌现时机与输出门控的响应;并采用systematic-outlier分析揭示MA的机制性生命周期,提出以MA取消时序为核心的解释框架。

Result: 发现两种架构对齐的MA形态:PAS(全注意力层前尖峰)和ISP(线性注意力层间的平台)。全注意力变密时,PAS通过ISP连通,最终恢复全注意力LLM的稳定MA形态。门控实验表明全注意力输出门控强烈衰减MA绝对幅度但不消除层间组织,去除GDN门控仅产生适度放大。机制分析支持MA取消时序主导的生命周期解释。

Conclusion: 本文提出了统一的MA生命周期理论,指出MA形态由MA的取消时序决定,其组织规律在广范围架构和规模上普适存在。这一发现为混合架构LLM的设计与优化提供了新的理论视角,且该视角在纯全注意力极限下与既有观察一致。

Abstract: We present the first systematic study of Massive activations (MAs) in layer-interleaved HLA LLMs and uncover two architecture-aligned morphologies: MAs consistently spike immediately before full attention layers, forming pre-attention spikes (PAS), and can persist through intervening linear attention layers, giving rise to inter-spike plateaus (ISP). As full attention becomes denser, successive PAS become increasingly connected through ISP, ultimately recovering the stable MA morphology of full attention LLMs. We establish the recurrence of this organization across five linear attention architectures, six hybridization configurations, five data domains, and representative open-source hybrid models spanning 1.2B to 397B total parameters. Controlled pretraining of GDN-based hybrids at scales up to 1.3B shows that both morphologies emerge early and respond asymmetrically to output gating: full attention output gating strongly attenuates their absolute magnitudes without eliminating their layerwise organization, whereas removing GDN gates yields comparatively modest amplification. Mechanistically, our systematic-outlier analysis supports a shared lifecycle account governed by the timing of MA cancellation. PAS follows a localized write-sink-cancel process, while the extended persistence of ISP is consistent with delayed cancellation. At the full attention limit, this account recovers the stable MA morphology characteristic of full attention LLMs. Our code is available at https://github.com/StartluxLabs/Massive-Activations-HLA.

</details>


### [152] [Information Abundance Paradox: Long-Context Training Undermines Parametric Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12218)
*Arda Uzunoglu,Benjamin van Durme,Daniel Khashabi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"信息丰裕悖论"：训练上下文中大量相关信息反而会降低模型将知识参数化编码的动机，导致模型更依赖上下文而非内部参数。研究发现，预训练和微调中增加上下文长度仅在中间最优值之前有益，之后性能持续下降，且这种效应源于长上下文提供了更低复杂度的解决方案，将梯度压力从前馈网络转向注意力模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型越来越倾向于使用长上下文进行训练和部署，隐含假设是更长的上下文训练只会通过提供更丰富的证据来帮助模型。作者质疑这一假设，认为需要系统研究上下文窗口如何塑造模型的学习模式，即在参数化内化与情境化之间的权衡，从而揭示长上下文训练可能带来的负面影响。

Method: 通过大规模预训练和指令微调实验，系统改变上下文窗口长度和训练时上下文的丰富程度，评估语言建模、自然语言理解和闭卷多项选择问答的性能；同时通过梯度分析（区分前馈网络与注意力模块的梯度压力）和因果干预实验来揭示长上下文导致性能下降的机制。

Result: 预训练中，增加上下文窗口仅在达到中间最优值之前提升语言建模、NLU和闭卷MCQA性能，之后性能持续下降；微调中，更多任务相关的训练上下文在测试时有支持上下文的场景提升性能，但在无上下文或误导性上下文时降低鲁棒性；机制上，信息丰富的长上下文将梯度从存储参数化知识的前馈网络转向注意力模块，从而增加推理时的上下文依赖。

Conclusion: "信息丰裕悖论"成立：即使高质量长上下文数据充足，向近乎无限上下文扩展并非简单地提供更多数据即可。训练上下文的信息丰裕度会改变模型的学习策略，过于丰富的上下文导致模型"懒惰"于参数化知识内化，降低其对上下文缺失或误导时的鲁棒性，这对长上下文语言模型的训练策略具有重要启示。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly trained and deployed with long contexts that span documents, code repositories, and interaction histories. This scaling reflects the implicit assumption that training on longer contexts will only help the model by exposing it to richer evidence. We challenge this view by studying how the context window shapes a model's mode of learning, shifting it between parametric internalization and contextualization. We propose the Information Abundance Paradox, which hypothesizes that abundant relevant information in the training context can reduce the incentive to encode that information parametrically, thereby increasing reliance on context. In pretraining with long documents, increasing the context window improves language modeling, natural language understanding, and closed-book MCQA only up to an intermediate optimum, after which performance consistently declines. In supervised fine-tuning, more task-relevant train-time context improves performance with supporting context, but reduces robustness when context is absent or misleading at test time. Our analysis suggests that this behavior arises when longer context provides a lower complexity solution. Mechanistically, training with informative context shifts gradient pressure from feed-forward networks, often linked to parametric knowledge, toward attention modules, and causal interventions show that this shift increases reliance on context during inference. Overall, these findings support the Information Abundance Paradox and suggest that scaling toward near-infinite context is not simply a matter of supplying more data, even when high-quality long-context data is abundant.

</details>


### [153] [One Frozen Simulator Is Not Enough: Simulator Collapse in Multi-Agent RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12253)
*Simon Yu,Nicholas Tomlin,Marwa Abdulhai,Ximing Lu,Derek Chong,Abe Hou,Dilara Soylu,Sergey Levine,Christopher D. Manning,Weiyan Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出多智能体强化学习中用单一LLM模拟用户会导致"模拟器坍缩"，使策略无法泛化；提出了推理时的Verbalized Sampling和训练时的Co-Training两种互补方案，在三个基准和人工评测中分别将成功率提升最多9%和14%，并开源了SCOPE框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机交互的多智能体强化学习通常依赖单个大型语言模型来模拟用户行为，但作者发现这种方法在系统性地泛化失败，根源在于模拟器坍缩——模拟器LLM发生模式坍缩后，基于其训练的策略会过拟合到利用主导模式的狭窄策略上，导致对未见过的模拟器和真实用户迁移效果差。

Method: 作者首先从理论上形式化描述了模拟器坍缩问题。随后提出两种互补方案：(1) 推理时方案Verbalized Sampling，通过从口头化的响应分布中采样来拓宽模拟器行为，减轻模式坍缩；(2) 训练时方案Co-Training，联合优化策略对抗一组可训练的模拟器种群，防止策略过拟合到任何一个模拟器的模式上。此外开源了SCOPE框架，支持种群协同训练的多智能体强化学习。

Result: 在Persuasion for Good、τ²-bench和CooperBench三个多轮基准上验证：Verbalized Sampling相比单一模拟器RL在保留集上最多提升9%成功率，Co-Training进一步将提升推向14%；人工研究显示对真实用户也有相当的提升。两种方案都保持了单一模拟器RL下会坍缩的策略多样性。

Conclusion: 训练环境的多样性（而非仅策略本身）对于多轮强化学习泛化到真实世界部署至关重要。作者开源SCOPE框架以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Multi-agent reinforcement learning for human-AI interaction typically relies on a single large language model to simulate user behavior. We show that this approach systematically fails to generalize, and trace the failure to simulator collapse: because the simulator LLM is mode-collapsed, an LLM policy trained against it overfits to narrow strategies that exploit the simulator's dominant mode, and such a policy transfers poorly to unseen simulators and real users. We formalize this collapse theoretically and propose two complementary solutions, one at inference time and one at training time. The inference-time solution, Verbalized Sampling, broadens the simulator's behavior by sampling from a verbalized response distribution, reducing mode collapse. The training-time solution, Co-Training, jointly optimizes the policy against a population of trainable simulators, preventing it from overfitting to any single simulator's mode. We validate both solutions on three multi-turn benchmarks: Persuasion for Good, $τ^2$-bench, and CooperBench. Verbalized Sampling improves held-out success by up to 9% over single-simulator RL, and Co-Training pushes gains further to 14%; the human study shows similar gain on real users. Both solutions preserve the policy diversity that collapses under single-simulator RL. To support further work in this direction, we release SCOPE, an open-source framework for Population Co-Training multi-agent RL. More broadly, our results suggest that the diversity of the training environment, not only the policy, is critical to the generalization of multi-turn RL to real-world deployment.

</details>


### [154] [A Cascaded Unsupervised-Supervised NLP Pipeline for Detecting Accusatory Language in Public Procurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12269)
*Bryan Torres,Daniel Riofrío,José Vega-Sánchez,Nathaly Orozco,Carla Parra,Karen Rosero,Felipe Grijalva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个混合建模框架，结合无监督聚类和GMM与有监督分类（Random Forest），利用Word2Vec、LLaMA和RoBERTa语义嵌入分析厄瓜多尔公共采购系统（SOCE）的参与者评论元数据，以检测潜在的违规采购流程。实验表明，领域训练的Word2Vec嵌入与GMM聚类及Random Forest分类器的组合在高类不平衡下仍能达到高精确率和召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共采购涉及大量财政资源，需要持续的审计、控制和监督。然而，利益相关者的评论和公开的政府数据往往未被充分利用，尽管这些数据有潜力揭示程序性违规行为。现有方法忽略了这些元数据中的语义信息。

Method: 提出一个集成无监督聚类（Gaussian Mixture Models）和有监督分类（Random Forest）的混合建模框架，嵌入自然语言处理流水线中。使用Word2Vec、LLaMA和RoBERTa生成语义嵌入，先进行GMM无监督聚类以发现潜在模式，再通过有监督分类识别指控性或举报式评论。

Result: 实验结果表明，领域训练的Word2Vec嵌入与GMM聚类及Random Forest分类器的组合在严重类不平衡条件下仍能达到高精确率和召回率。

Conclusion: 轻量级、领域自适应的NLP架构能够有效支持公共采购系统中的风险识别和透明度提升，无需大规模计算基础设施。

Abstract: Public procurement involves the allocation of substantial financial resources; therefore, continuous oversight through audits, controls, and monitoring mechanisms is essential. However, stakeholder comments and publicly available government data are often underutilized, despite their potential to reveal procedural irregularities. To address this gap, this paper analyzes metadata from Ecuador's Sistema Oficial de Contratación Pública (SOCE, Official Public Procurement System), with particular emphasis on participant comments generated during the pre-contractual phase. We propose a hybrid modeling framework that integrates unsupervised clustering and supervised classification within a natural language processing (NLP) pipeline to uncover latent patterns and detect potentially irregular procurement processes. Semantic embeddings are generated using Word2Vec, LLaMA, and RoBERTa, followed by Gaussian Mixture Models (GMMs) for unsupervised clustering. A supervised classification stage is then applied to identify accusatory or whistleblowing-style comments. Experimental results show that the combination of domain-trained Word2Vec embeddings, GMM-based clustering, and a Random Forest classifier achieves high precision and recall, even under severe class imbalance. These findings demonstrate that lightweight, domain-adapted NLP architectures can effectively support risk identification and enhance transparency in public procurement systems without requiring large-scale computational infrastructure.

</details>


### [155] [Structural Silence: When AI Infrastructure Fails Speakers of Underrepresented Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12278)
*Avijit Roy,Proma Roy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过孟加拉语这一案例，揭示AI教育工具在欠发达地区部署中面临的结构性障碍：网络内容缺口、训练语料不平衡、分词惩罚和连接性排斥四大互锁失效机制，主张将数据稀缺理解为结构性障碍而非孤立的技术局限，并提出以离线优先设计作为面向公平的基础设施策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI教育工具被广泛宣传为弥补欠发达社区资源缺口的可扩展方案，但其底层基础设施（训练语料、分词方案、评估基准、部署架构）在模型训练之前就已系统性排斥少数语言使用者。论文以孟加拉语为例，试图揭示这些被忽视的结构性障碍。

Method: 论文通过孟加拉语的典型案例，识别并分析了四个相互关联的失败层面：(1) 网络内容覆盖缺口（孟加拉语占全球人口近4%但网络内容不足0.5%）；(2) 主流多语言语料库中英语与孟加拉语67:1的训练token差异；(3) 孟加拉语音节文字带来的分词惩罚（更高的token增殖率放大数据劣势）；(4) 连接性排斥（农村个人互联网普及率36.5%对城市71.4%）。

Result: 论文识别出四大结构性障碍的具体量化证据，揭示了这些失败源于长期的资源分配决策、机构优先级和未以少数语言为中心的设计默认值，并提出数据集稀缺应被视为结构性障碍而非孤立的技术限制。

Conclusion: 论文主张将离线优先设计作为面向公平的基础设施策略，并为语言学和AI研究提出了减少这些结构性不平等的研究方向。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence tools for education and language support are increasingly framed as scalable responses to access gaps in under-resourced communities. Yet the infrastructure underlying these tools, including training corpora, tokenization schemes, evaluation benchmarks, and deployment architectures, can systematically disadvantage speakers of underrepresented languages before a model is trained.
  This paper examines these structural barriers through Bengali, one of the world's most widely spoken languages, focusing on AI-assisted education in low-connectivity environments. We identify four interlocking failures: a severe web presence gap, with Bengali accounting for less than 0.5% of global web content despite representing nearly 4% of the global population; a 67:1 training-token deficit between English and Bengali in major multilingual corpora; a tokenization penalty associated with Bengali's alphasyllabary script that compounds the data deficit through higher token fertility; and connectivity exclusion, with individual internet penetration at 36.5% in rural areas compared with 71.4% in urban areas.
  These failures reflect longstanding resource-allocation decisions, institutional priorities, and design defaults that did not center underrepresented languages in mainstream AI development. We argue that dataset scarcity should be understood as a structural barrier rather than an isolated technical limitation, and that offline-first design should be treated as an equity-oriented infrastructure strategy. We conclude with directions for linguistics and AI research aimed at reducing these structural inequalities.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

### [156] [A Geodesic Cut-Cell Prior for Neural Skinning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11272)
*Wenchao Ma,Surya Dwarakanath,Yizhak Ben-Shabat,Dario Kneubühler,Haomiao Jiang,Sharon X. Huang,Hsueh-Ti Derek Liu*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出cut-cell skinning，一种基于图逼近体积测地距离的几何先验，用于增强数据驱动的蒙皮权重生成。该方法相比基于优化的求解器可实现数量级的加速，对cage或voxel方法常见的拓扑伪影具有鲁棒性，集成到现有神经蒙皮模型中可取得一致的性能提升和最先进的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的蒙皮权重生成方法虽能产生高质量的蒙皮权重，但缺乏经典几何方法的泛化能力。作者希望通过一个几何先验来弥合这一差距，该先验既能在野网格上稳健计算，又能高效适应大规模机器学习工作流。

Method: 关键思想是基于图的方法快速逼近体积测地距离，这源于经典蒙皮权重计算中测地距离的重要性。该方法作为几何先验，可注入数据驱动的蒙皮权重生成模型。通过图的近似替代基于优化的体积测地距离求解，实现速度的大幅提升，同时避免了cage或voxel方法中常见的拓扑伪影问题。

Result: 相比基于优化的求解器实现数量级的加速；将先验集成到最新神经蒙皮模型后，在现有方法上取得一致性改进，达到最先进的性能水平。

Conclusion: cut-cell skinning作为一种高效且鲁棒的几何先验，能有效增强数据驱动蒙皮权重生成的泛化能力，与神经蒙皮模型结合后显著提升结果。

Abstract: We introduce cut-cell skinning, a geometric prior designed to augment data-driven skinning weight generation. While data-driven methods show promise in producing high-quality skinning weights, they often lack the generalizability of classic geometric approaches. To bridge this gap, we propose a geometric prior that can be robustly computed for in-the-wild meshes and is efficient for large-scale machine learning workflows. The key idea of our cut-cell skinning is a fast graph-based approximation of the volumetric geodesics distances, motivated by their importance in classic skinning weight computation. Our method achieves orders of magnitude speedup compared to optimization-based solvers and remains resilient to topological artifacts common in cage- or voxel-based alternatives. We demonstrate the efficacy of the cut-cell skinning prior by integrating it into recent neural skinning models, showing consistent improvements across existing methods and achieving state-of-the-art results. Project page: https://wenchao-m.github.io/CutCell.github.io/

</details>


### [157] [MVFM-3DAD: Multi-view Flow Matching for 3D Anomaly Detection via Density Proxy Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12148)
*Liangwei Li,Lin Liu,Jing Zhang,Xiaohui Du,Ruqian Hao,Xinwei Li,Hanzhe Liang,Juanxiu Liu*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出MVFM-3DAD，一种基于流的3D异常检测框架，通过双向几何投影器将点云转换为多视图表示，并利用流引导的密度代理估计器评估每个视图特征的密度，无需输入重建或Jacobian计算，在Real3D-AD和MVTec3D-AD上优于现有最强方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D异常检测方法主要分为基于记忆库检索和基于重建两类。记忆库方法受限于存储正常特征的覆盖范围，重建方法可能学习恒等捷径使异常输入也能被良好重建，这两类方法均存在固有局限。因此需要一种密度导向的方法，直接评估测试样本是否符合学习到的正常数据分布。

Method: 提出MVFM-3DAD流式框架，包含两个核心组件：1）双向几何投影器（BGP），其前向过程将不规则点云转换为结构化的多视图表示；2）流引导密度代理估计器（FDPE），为每个视图特征估计参考密度，然后通过BGP的反向过程将这些多视图密度估计映射回对应的3D点。基于此，通过异常特征的终态正态性来识别异常。该公式无需输入重建或显式Jacobian计算。

Result: 在Real3D-AD和MVTec3D-AD两个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，MVFM-3DAD的性能超越了最强的竞争方法。

Conclusion: MVFM-3DAD通过将3D异常检测重构为正常数据分布上的密度代理估计问题，有效克服了记忆库方法和重建方法的固有缺陷，提供了一种简单高效的异常评分机制，并在两个主流基准上取得了最优性能。代码已开源。

Abstract: In 3D anomaly detection (3DAD), most existing methods rely on Memory bank retrieval or reconstruction. However, memory-based methods are constrained by the coverage of stored normal features, while reconstruction-based methods may learn identity shortcuts that also reconstruct anomalous inputs well. These limitations motivate a density-oriented approach that evaluates whether a test sample follows the learned normal distribution. To this end, we propose MVFM-3DAD, a flow-based framework that reframes 3DAD as density proxy estimation over the normal data distribution. MVFM-3DAD introduces a Bidirectional Geometric Projector (BGP), whose forward process converts irregular point clouds into structured multi-view representations. The Flow-guided Density Proxy Estimator (FDPE) estimates a reference density for each view feature, after which the backward process of BGP maps these multi-view density estimates to their corresponding 3D points. Building on it, anomalous features can be identified by their terminal normality. Unlike conventional flow-based likelihood estimation, our formulation requires neither input reconstruction nor explicit Jacobian evaluation, yielding a simple and efficient anomaly-scoring mechanism. Extensive experiments show that MVFM-3DAD outperforms the strongest competing methods on Real3D-AD and MVTec3D-AD. Code is available at https://github.com/lil-wayne-0319/MV3D-AD

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [Dynamic Governance of Multi-LLM Agent Systems for Collaborative Conversational Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11207)
*Alexander Liss,Nicholas Desmond,Santiago Gil Gallego*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨当两个目标对立的LLM代理在多轮交互中缺乏共享目标函数时会产生"崩溃"而非竞争的问题，并提出一个控制理论治理层——体验编排器（EO）来替代缺失的目标函数。在60,000次模拟中，EO将高意图顾问联系率提升32个百分点（78.1% vs 46.1%），其中上下文赌博机（CB）的变体选择解释了97%的因子间结果方差，证明治理策略而非环境初始条件决定轨迹终点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当两个目标结构对立的LLM代理进行多轮交互时，缺少共享目标函数会导致系统"崩溃"：访客屈服、站点代理停止变化策略、对话在双方目标均未达成时终止。作者想知道控制理论治理层能否替代缺失的目标函数来维持系统功能性。

Method: 在模拟金融服务环境中构建体验编排器（EO），通过三种机制治理联合轨迹：1) 上下文赌博机（CB）从真实网络分析数据校准内容臂选择；2) PID控制器通过动态模式约束强制执行行为一致性；3) POMDP信念追踪器维持访客意图的概率模型。在60,000次LLM对LLM模拟中进行评估。

Result: 相较朴素LLM对照组（46.1%），EO实现了78.1%的高意图顾问联系率（+32个百分点提升）。CB变体选择解释了97%的因子间结果方差，确认治理策略而非环境初始条件决定结果。人物层面分析发现两种范式：没有自然转化倾向的访客依赖治理层才能实现功能性；已接近对齐的访客仅靠LLM的移情默认行为已足够。

Conclusion: 治理层可以替代缺失的共享目标函数来维持对抗性多代理系统的功能性，但其有效性限于LLM到LLM模拟环境。PID控制器未针对真实人类不可预测性校准，在真实流量上验证EO是关键的下一步。

Abstract: When two LLM agents with structurally opposed objectives interact across multiple turns, the absence of a shared goal function produces not competition but collapse: the visitor capitulates, the site agent stops varying its approach, and the conversation terminates without achieving either agent's stated objective. This paper asks whether a control-theoretic governance layer can substitute for that missing goal function. The Experience Orchestrator (EO) addresses this in a simulated financial services environment where a site agent guides a visitor toward advisor contact while the visitor maintains psychologically realistic resistance. EO governs the joint trajectory through three mechanisms: a Contextual Bandit (CB) that selects content arms calibrated from real-world web analytics, a PID controller that enforces behavioral consistency via dynamic schema constraints, and a POMDP belief tracker that maintains a probabilistic model of visitor intent. Across 60,000 simulations, EO achieves a +32 percentage point lift in high-intent advisor contact rate (78.1% vs. 46.1% over a naive LLM control), with CB variant selection accounting for 97% of between-factor outcome variance -- confirming that the governance policy, not environmental initial conditions, determines where trajectories end up. Persona-level analysis reveals two distinct regimes: for visitors with no natural inclination toward conversion, the governance layer is the difference between a functional system and a non-functional one; for visitors already near alignment, a naive LLM's empathetic defaults are largely sufficient. All findings are conditional on LLM-to-LLM simulation. The PID controller has not been calibrated against real human unpredictability, and validating EO on live traffic is the critical next step.

</details>


### [159] [Distribird: Literature-Informed Prior Distribution Design for Bayesian Model Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11210)
*Patrik P. Süli,György Eigner,Roland Hollós*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Distribird是一个智能体驱动的Web应用，用于自动化贝叶斯模型参数先验分布的构建过程。它通过多智能体流水线从科学文献中搜索、提取和加权参数值，并通过AIC模型选择拟合概率分布，当文献不可用时回退到合理的无信息先验，同时提供可追溯的证据和置信度报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于过程的模型贝叶斯校准需要每个参数的先验分布，但研究人员几乎总是使用均匀先验，因为从科学文献中构建信息性先验既耗时又需要领域和统计两方面的专业知识。现有的做法缺乏可扩展的自动化解决方案。

Method: Distribird采用多智能体流水线：搜索文献、提取报告值并按领域相关性加权、通过AIC模型选择拟合概率分布。无文献时回退到无信息先验。内置有效性检查层拒绝越界请求，所有LLM调用在本地运行，仅生成的搜索词到达公共文献数据库。评估在10个科学领域的24个参数上进行，比较三个开源模型与单提示LLM基线。

Result: 在先验质量上，完整流水线与单提示LLM基线相当。但Distribird的每个先验都追溯到具体论文和数值，内置有效性层在30个模型-参数组合中有11个拒绝产生无依据先验，而单提示基线在这些情况下会给出自信但无根据的先验。所有语言模型调用均在本地运行，保护了未发表的数据隐私。

Conclusion: 论文主张对于科学用途而言，可追溯性、有效性和数据隐私比边际的点估计精度改进更重要。Distribird为自动化先验构建提供了一个兼顾透明度、可靠性和隐私保护的解决方案。

Abstract: Bayesian calibration of process-based models requires a prior distribution for each model parameter. Despite decades of methodological work, researchers almost always fall back on uniform priors. The main reason is that building informative priors from scientific literature is slow and needs both domain and statistical expertise. We present \textbf{Distribird}, an agentic web application that automates this process. Given a parameter name, physical description, and domain context, Distribird deploys a multi-agent pipeline that searches the literature, extracts and weights reported values by domain relevance, and fits a probability distribution via AIC model selection. When no literature is available, the system falls back to sensible uninformative alternatives, and clearly reports both the evidence behind and the confidence level of every prior it produces. It is designed for the problems where the models have physically interpretable parameters, where domain knowledge exists in the published literature. We evaluate the tool on 24~parameters across 10 scientific domains comparing three open-weight models (Qwen3.6 27B, Gemma 4 31B, Mistral Small 4 119B) with a single-prompt LLM baseline. On prior quality the full pipeline \emph{matches} this baseline. Every prior is traced to the specific papers and values from which it was constructed; a built-in validity layer declines to produce priors for out-of-scope requests, whereas the single-prompt baseline returns confident but unfounded priors for them in 11 of 30~model--parameter cases; and every language-model call runs locally, so no parameter description or unpublished modelling detail is transmitted to a third-party LLM provider (only generated search terms reach the public literature databases). For scientific use, we argue these properties matter more than a marginal improvement in point-estimate accuracy.

</details>


### [160] [A Forced-Structure Reduction and Verifiable Bounds for Conway's 99-Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11211)
*Aalok Thakkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种由自主AI研究代理对Conway 99-graph问题（即srg(99,14,1,2)是否存在）进行的系统性、可完全复现的攻击。主要贡献包括：证明了循环图在Z/99上最多满足68.0%的约束；通过λ=1和μ=2的强制结构约简将存在性问题归结为84个顶点上的12-正则图问题；验证了指定自同构轨道存在性框架；最终最佳验证构件达到69.43%的满足率，并提供证据表明这是鲁棒的前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Conway的99-graph问题是一个长期未解的经典组合学开放问题，涉及强正则图srg(99,14,1,2)的存在性。传统穷举搜索在如此大的参数空间上计算不可行，因此需要创新的系统性方法。作者希望通过自主AI研究代理进行可复现的攻击，并利用部分信用评分指标，在无法完全解决的情况下也能提供有价值的中间结果和结构性洞察。

Method: 作者采用多管齐下的方法：(1)对Z/99上的循环图和其他阶为99的阿贝尔群进行穷举证明，确定约束满足率的上限为68.0%；(2)利用参数强制结构——λ=1使每个邻域成为完美匹配，μ=2使外层顶点与非匹配邻域对双射——将问题约简为84个顶点上的12-正则图，编码为CP-SAT求解，通过恢复唯一的srg(9,4,1,2)进行验证；(3)建立指定的自同构轨道存在性框架（无不动点和单不动点作用），在srg(9,4,1,2)和Paley图srg(13,6,2,3)上验证；(4)通过十四种不同方法寻找最佳可行构件，最佳达到69.43%的满足率。

Result: 四个可验证的贡献均已完成：(1)穷举证明循环图和非循环阿贝尔群情形下约束满足率上限为68.0%；(2)成功建立约简体系并在小规模图上验证正确性；(3)自同构轨道框架在已知图上通过了验证；(4)最佳构件达到69.43%满足率，且十四种独立方法均未超过此值，表明这是鲁棒的计算前沿。

Conclusion: 该工作虽然未直接解决Conway 99-graph问题，但提供了重要的结构性约简和计算边界。关键的洞见是：任何低于4950（总约束数）的可证明上界都是非存在性证明，因此69.43%的最佳构件及其鲁棒前沿与该开放问题深刻纠缠。这项工作展示了自主AI研究代理在推进经典数学开放问题方面的潜力，其全部结果均可复现。

Abstract: Conway's 99-graph problem asks whether a strongly regular graph with parameters $\mathrm{srg}(99,14,1,2)$ exists. We report a systematic, fully reproducible attack by an autonomous AI research agent, scored under the track's partial-credit metric. Our verifiable contributions are: (1) an exhaustive proof that no circulant graph on $\mathbb{Z}/99$ satisfies more than $3366/4950=68.0\%$ of the constraints ($33$ of $49$ difference-classes), with the same ceiling for the other abelian group of order $99$; (2) a forced-structure reduction: $λ=1$ makes each neighbourhood a perfect matching and $μ=2$ puts the outer vertices in bijection with non-matched neighbour-pairs, collapsing existence to a $12$-regular graph on $84$ vertices, encoded for CP-SAT and validated by recovering the unique $\mathrm{srg}(9,4,1,2)$; (3) a validated prescribed-automorphism orbit-existence framework (fixed-point-free and single-fixed-point actions, checked on $\mathrm{srg}(9,4,1,2)$ and the Paley graph $\mathrm{srg}(13,6,2,3)$), and (4) a best verified artifact at $69.43\%$, with evidence that this is a robust frontier (fourteen distinct methods, none exceeding it) entangled with the open question, since any provable bound below $4950$ is a non-existence proof.

</details>


### [161] [Detecting a Route Flip Is Easier Than Knowing Whether to Fix It: Causal Route-Mediated Damage in Quantized Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11212)
*Parvel Gu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了Top-k混合专家模型（MoE）路由的不连续性：模拟的4-bit KV缓存量化扰动会推动token跨越决策边界，改变被激活的专家。论文不提出新的缓解方法，而是通过因果装置、实证发现和检测极限结果，量化"路由介导"的量化损伤占比（约1/3），并发现存在"符号不可分离"的经验屏障——即从局部推理可观测的路由统计量中无法预测翻转损失的正负号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE路由的离散决策（Top-k）天然不连续，这意味着数值扰动（如KV缓存量化的读噪声）可能改变路由结果，导致损伤以"间接/路由介导"的方式产生。现有的量化误差分析方法通常假设损伤是逐个参数独立的，忽视了路由边界效应。作者旨在提供严格的因果归因框架来区分"路由介导损伤"与"直接激活损伤"，并检验部署场景下能否通过可观测的路由统计量来检测和修复这种损伤。

Method: 提出四部分分析装置：(1) 路由介导分数（RMF），定价量化损伤中经由路由改变的那部分占比；(2) token级归因，按机制分解损伤来源；(3) 预注册探针，跨三个架构验证发现的一致性；(4) 部署路由器余量（router margin）作为检测统计量。实验在OLMoE-1B-7B上进行4-bit KV量化（含pilot、发现、过程复制和预注册重执行四轮），并做受控的相同checkpoint标志翻转实验来区分gate归一化约定与路由可恢复性。此外用真实int4 KV内核验证假量化剂量响应曲线。

Result: 在OLMoE-1B-7B的4-bit KV量化中，约1/3的损伤来自路由介导（RMF≈0.31，各轮次估计 0.231~0.313）。路由器余量能检测到翻转发生（AUC 0.772），但无法区分有害翻转与有益翻转（表现接近随机）。在测试的所有局部可观测路由器统计量中，没有一个能预测翻转的损失符号超过随机水平——这构成"经验性收益检测屏障"，限制了选择性修复的可行性。符号翻转税和符号不可分离性跨模型成立；clean-reference补救方法的收益因架构而异；gate的归一化约定只是损伤量级的调节因素，而非路由可恢复性机制。真实int4内核的结果与假量化剂量曲线兼容但功效不足（95%CI包含零）。

Conclusion: 路由介导的量化损伤在MoE中占比显著（约1/3），不可忽视；但检测到"翻转发生"与预测"翻转的好坏"之间存在根本性能力差异——前者可解（AUC 0.772），后者在局部可观测统计量族内不可解（随机水平）。这为"选择性修复"设置了经验屏障，意味着修复策略不能依赖局部观测的单一翻转信号，而需寻找更强信号的架构或绕过局部特征的设计。论文遵循预注册流程，诚实报告了未达显著性（严格排除不可能性）的结果，证明假量化与真实内核之间仅排除严重分歧而非独立复制。

Abstract: Top-k Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) routing is discontinuous, so a deployment-motivated numerical disturbance -- simulated 4-bit KV-cache quantization read by a protected BF16 gate -- pushes tokens across decision boundaries and flips which experts fire. This paper proposes no new mitigation; it supplies a causal apparatus, empirical findings, and a detection-limit result. A four-run apparatus prices the route-mediated fraction (RMF) of quantization damage, a token-level attribution decomposes it by mechanism, and pre-registered probes carry the findings across three architectures. On OLMoE-1B-7B at 4-bit KV (pilot), about a third of the damage is routing-mediated: RMF ~ 0.31 (discovery 0.31 [0.20, 0.41]; process-replicated mean 0.313 +/- 0.020; pre-registered re-execution 0.231). The deployable router margin detects that a flip occurred (AUC 0.772) but cannot tell a harmful flip from a helpful one (at chance): among the tested local, inference-observable router statistics we find no predictor of a flip's loss sign above chance -- an empirical benefit-detection barrier bounding selective repair restricted to this feature family. The signed-flip tax and sign-inseparability carry cross-model; the clean-reference remedy's payout is architecture-modulated; a controlled same-checkpoint flag-swap re-scopes the gate's normalization convention to a damage-magnitude moderator, not a route-recoverability mechanism. A real int4 KV kernel yields a fraction compatible with the fake-quant dose curve but underpowered (95% CI [-0.111, 0.394] includes zero) -- ruling out gross disagreement, not an independent replication. Hypotheses, thresholds, and evaluations were pre-registered before measurement, with misses reported; a pre-registered held-out read replicates the partition and the near-cancelling tax out of sample, while the strict impossibility exclusion narrowly misses.

</details>


### [162] [Poor Man's Agentic Modeling: Simulating Large LLM-Agent Societies on a Laptop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11215)
*Igor Itkin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出将统计物理方法应用于LLM多智能体社会模拟：用低参数代理模型替代昂贵的LLM智能体，通过少量廉价查询拟合，从而在笔记本上运行任意N规模的社会模拟，并引入[交互阶数×记忆]分类法来预测替代误差的N趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟大量LLM智能体的社会行为计算成本高昂，但研究问题通常是宏观层面的（相变行为、风格化事实、与智能体数量N的标度关系），而非单个智能体的认知细节。需要一种低成本的方法来模拟大规模LLM社会。

Method: 将每个LLM智能体替换为低参数模型，从几百到几千次廉价查询中拟合得到；然后可在笔记本上以任意N运行社会模拟。引入[交互阶数×记忆]分类法，将感知和记忆映射到有效理论和代理误差的预测N趋势。在EconAgent及其他七个LLM模拟的实现上验证，智能体决策从真实的LLM（主要为DeepSeek）查询克隆而来。

Result: 预测的误差趋势在各单元中成立；两个被推翻的预测均涉及强饱和响应，并可追溯至其曲率，但即便无自由参数，理论也能定量匹配这两个反例。

Conclusion: 该方法能以极低成本运行大规模LLM社会模拟，且其有效性在模拟运行前即可由智能体感知特性决定；分类法能较好地预测代理误差的规模趋势，理论框架同样解释了少数反例。

Abstract: Simulating societies of many large language model (LLM) agents is expensive, yet the questions asked of such simulations are usually macroscopic: phase behaviour, stylised facts, and scaling with the number of agents $N$, not the cognition of any single agent. We turn a statistical-physics observation into a method: replace each LLM agent by a low-parameter model fitted from a few hundred to a few thousand cheap queries, then run the society at any $N$ on a laptop. Whether this works is decided before the simulation runs, chiefly by what each agent perceives. We introduce an [interaction order x memory] taxonomy that maps perception and memory to an effective theory and a predicted $N$-trend of the surrogate error. We validate it on a faithful reimplementation of the LLM macroeconomy EconAgent and seven further named LLM simulations, with agent decisions cloned from genuine LLM elicitations (primarily DeepSeek) for a few dollars; the predicted error trends hold cell by cell, and the two refuted predictions, both on a strongly saturating response and traced to its curvature, are themselves matched quantitatively by the theory with no free parameters.

</details>


### [163] [AutoWorldModel-Bench: A State-Centric Benchmark for Automated World-Model Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11216)
*Marjan Moodi,Xuankang Zhu,Fernando De Mesentier Silva,Harold Chaput,Mohammad Reza Taesiri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoWorldModel-Bench，一个闭环基准测试，用于评估前沿编码智能体作为自主研究者改进世界模型的能力。在8个游戏环境和统一结构化状态表示下，64个会话中63个智能体成功改进基线，91%的会话中关键修改是研究型改动而非超参数调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界建模领域架构、训练目标和状态表示之间复杂交互，缺乏统一解决方案。现有智能体基准测试主要关注"按规格工程"任务（改进方向预先指定），而缺少评估AI编码智能体在开放式研究环境中的能力。作者希望填补这一空白，构建一个不预设改进方向的基准。

Method: 构建AutoWorldModel-Bench基准：(1) 覆盖8个游戏环境，使用统一的张量格式传递游戏地面真值实体状态；(2) 将动力学建模与感知解耦，支持分钟级迭代；(3) 采用闭环评估框架，前沿编码智能体（Codex-5.4和Claude Opus 4.6）在固定计算预算下自主改进给定的世界模型起始代码；(4) 对智能体的改进进行分类，区分研究型修改与超参数调优。

Result: 64个会话中，智能体在63个中成功改进了起点模型；91%的会话中，获胜编辑是非平凡的研究型修改——包括新目标函数、新表示、新滚动程序或架构变更，而非简单的超参数调整。Codex-5.4和Claude Opus 4.6均展现出超越工程任务的开放式研究能力。

Conclusion: AutoWorldModel-Bench为评估前沿编码智能体在开放式研究任务中的能力提供了一个有效基准，表明当前AI编码智能体已能够在未预先指定改进方向的情况下进行自主研究型探索，不同于传统的按规格工程基准。

Abstract: World modeling is an unsettled field: architectures, training objectives, and state representations interact in complex ways, and no single recipe dominates across environments. This makes it an ideal testbed for AI coding agents acting as autonomous researchers--a setting in which the improvement direction is not specified in advance, unlike the engineering-to-spec tasks that dominate current agent benchmarks. We introduce AutoWorldModel-Bench, a closed-loop benchmark in which frontier coding agents autonomously improve a provided world-model starter under a fixed compute budget. The benchmark spans eight game environments under a unified structured-state representation--ground-truth entity state extracted from each game and consumed through a shared tensor format--which isolates dynamics modeling from perception and enables minutes-per-run iteration. Across 64 sessions, Codex-5.4 and Claude Opus 4.6 improve their starter on 63; in 91% of sessions the winning edit is a non-trivial research-style modification--a new objective, representation, rollout procedure, or architectural change--rather than a hyperparameter tweak. Our benchmark offers a setting in which frontier coding agents can be evaluated on open-ended research rather than engineering-to-spec problems.

</details>


### [164] [MaSRead: Content-Addressed Reading of Replicated Latent Stores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11218)
*Carlos Baquero,Luís Brito,João Resende*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MaSRead通过内容寻址的方式，使独立智能体在潜空间中共享的键值缓存片段（通过无冲突复制数据类型合并）能够被后续未知查询可靠读取。它利用不透明键控标签集路由，在硬注意力掩码下解码片段，并通过词法连通性的图遍历满足多跳查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立智能体在潜空间中推理时可共享计算状态（键值缓存片段），但合并后的存储中同位片段相互干扰，导致同位（colocation）不等于可寻址（addressability）——后续在编码时未知的查询无法可靠读取合并后的缓存。这揭示了"可合并的复制存储"与"可寻址读取"之间的根本张力。

Method: MaSRead将读取定向到内容本身：(1) 从片段词元派生出不透明键控标签集用于路由；(2) 对每个选中片段在硬注意力掩码下解码，屏蔽其他片段；(3) 在词法连通性假设下，通过图遍历方式找到多跳查询所需的多个片段。合并采用无冲突复制数据类型（CRDT）以保证任意投递顺序或重复下收敛。

Result: 在链式、流水线、对称、中心枢纽和自然语言存储等多种拓扑下，MaSRead都能隔离地恢复已访问片段；在无关片段累积时仍保持有效性，并能迁移到另一模型家族。路由后，物化解码的工作量取决于片段长度而非总存储规模；端到端工作仍包含存储相关路由和每个访问片段的单次读取。

Conclusion: 复制的潜空间存储可通过内容寻址实现选择性可读，前提是所需片段在内容上连接到查询。但存在明确局限：词法路由可能遗漏无连接证据，答案组合受限于冻结的读者模型（frozen reader）。

Abstract: Independent agents that reason in latent space can share computed state as key-value cache fragments rather than text. Merged by a conflict-free replicated data type, these fragments form a store that converges under any delivery order or duplication. Yet a later query, unknown at encode time, cannot reliably read the merged cache: colocated fragments interfere, so colocation is not addressability. MaSRead addresses the read to content.
